2026 年 10 月 9 日 全9 頁

Indicators Update 
2026 年 8 月消費統計 
耐久財とサービスが弱く、総じて見れば前月から減少

経済調査部 エコノミスト 龐 鈞文

[要約] 
◼ 2026 年8 月の家計調査における二人以上世帯の実質消費支出は前月比＋0.1％と2 カ月
連続で増加した。 一方、複数の需要側統計を用いて補正した世帯消費動向指数（ CTI
ミクロ）で見た実質消費は同▲2.3％と 2 カ月ぶりに減少した。また、供給側統計の 1
つである商業動態統計では、CPI の財指数で実質化した小売販売額は同▲1.1％だった。
総じて見れば、8 月の個人消費は前月から減少したと判断される。 
◼ 先行きの個人消費は 底堅く推移するとみている 。名目賃金は春闘での高水準の賃上げ
や最低賃金の引き上げもあって上昇が続く見込みである。 一方、政府の物価高対策 の
効果の剝落などが物価を押し上げる要因となろう。加えて、原油の輸入価格は代替調
達もあって高止まりしており、幅広い分野の価格への波及を通じて個人消費を下押し
する可能性には引き続き注意が必要だ。

＜2026 年8 月の消費総括＞耐久財やサービスを中心に減少 
需要側統計である家計調査における二人以上世帯の実質消費支出は、前月比＋0.1％と2カ月
連続で小幅に増加した一方、複数の需要側統計を用いて補正した世帯消費動向指数（CTI ミク
ロ）で見た実質消費は同▲2.3％と 2 カ月ぶりに減少した（図表 1）。また、供給側統計の 1 つ
である商業動態統計では、CPI の財指数で実質化した小売販売額は同▲1.1％だった。総じて見
れば、2026年8月の個人消費は前月から減少したと判断される。

＜CTI ミクロ・家計調査（需要側）＞「交通・通信」などの7 費目が減少 
2026年 8 月の CTIミクロ（二人以上の世帯）を費目別に見ると、10 大費目1のうち、 「交通・
通信」 （前月比▲5.3％） 、「その他」 （同▲4.1％）、 「教養娯楽」 （同▲2.9％） 、 「教育」 （同▲
4.7％） 、「家具・家事用品」 （同▲4.4％） 、「被服及び履物」 （同▲3.7％） 、「保健医療」 （同▲
0.5％）の7費目が減少した。 
他方、「光熱・水道」 （前月比＋4.4％） 、「住居」 （同＋3.3％） 、「食料」 （同＋0.5％）の3費目
が増加した（図表2）。 
図表 2：実質世帯消費動向指数（CTI ミクロ）の前月比

（注）二人以上の世帯。総務省による季節調整値。シェアは 2025 年の数値。 
（出所）総務省統計より大和総研作成

算出している。

### Page 3

1

3 / 9

続いて、CTIミクロの10大費目に含まれる個別品目への支出について確認する。 
CTI ミクロにおける「交通・通信」は 2 カ月ぶりに減少した。自動車等関係費などへの支出
が減少した。「その他」は2カ月ぶりに減少した。諸雑費やこづかいなどへの支出が減少した。
「教養娯楽」は2カ月ぶりに減少した。教養娯楽サービスを中心に、教養娯楽用品などへの支出
が減少した。 「教育」は2 カ月連続で減少した。授業料等を中心に支出が減少した2。「家具・家
事用品」は2カ月ぶりに減少した。家庭用耐久財を中心に支出が減少した。「被服及び履物」は
2 カ月ぶりに減少した。洋服を中心に支出が減少した。「保健医療」は 2 カ月連続で減少した。
保健医療サービスを中心に支出が減少した。 
他方、「光熱・水道」は 2 カ月ぶりに増加した。電気代やガス代を中心に支出が拡大した。
「住居」は 2 カ月ぶりに増加した。家賃地代を中心に支出が拡大した。「食料」は 2 カ月連続で
増加した。酒類や野菜・海藻を中心に支出が拡大した。 
家計調査における二人以上世帯の実質消費支出は、前月比＋0.1％と2カ月連続で小幅に増加
した。ただし、振れの大きい項目を除いた、住居等を除くベースでは同▲1.7％と2カ月ぶりに
減少した（図表 3 上段左）。 
基礎的支出は前月比＋0.9％と、4 カ月ぶりに増加した。また、選択的支出は同＋1.3％と、2
カ月連続で増加した（いずれも大和総研による季節調整値、図表3 中段左）。 
消費支出を財・サービス別に見ると（大和総研による季節調整値、図表 3 中段右）、耐久財
（前月比＋8.2％）が4カ月ぶりに増加し、全体を押し上げた。非耐久財（同＋0.4％）も3カ月
ぶりに増加した。一方、サービス（同▲0.3％）と半耐久財（同▲0.1％）は減少した。

2 ただし、授業料等は児童や学生がいる一部の世帯以外はほとんど支出しないため、サンプルサイズが小さく
振れが大きい。

＜商業動態統計（供給側）＞小売販売額は名目・実質ともに2 カ月ぶりに減少 
2026 年 8 月の商業動態統計によると、名目小売販売額は前月比▲1.2％と 2 カ月ぶりに減少
した（図表 4、5） 。また、CPIの財指数で実質化した小売販売額は同▲1.1％だった。 
名目小売販売額を業種別に見ると、全ての業種が前月から減少した。「機械器具小売業」 （前
月比▲6.9％）は 2 カ月ぶりに減少した。「自動車小売業」 （同▲2.5％）は 4 カ月連続で減少し
た。8 月の新車販売台数（大和総研による季節調整値）は同▲7.6％と減少しており3、この結
果とも整合的だ。「燃料小売業」 （同▲1.8％）は2カ月ぶりに減少した。「飲食料品小売業」 （同
▲0.4％）は2カ月ぶりに減少した。コンビニの加工食品（同▲1.5％）が減少した。「織物・衣
服・身の回り品小売業」 （同▲1.8％）、「その他小売業」 （同▲0.2％）、「各種商品小売業」 （同
▲0.4％）はいずれも2カ月ぶりに減少した。

の小売業」 。

＜2026 年9 月の消費＞8 月から減少した可能性 
業界統計や個社データ、JCB 消費 NOW をもとに判断すると、2026 年 9 月の消費は 8 月から減
少したとみている4。財消費では、9 月前半の実績をもとに試算したスーパーの JCB 消費額（大
和総研による季節調整値）と家電の JCB 消費額（同）は減少した。一方、アパレルの売上高は
堅調に推移し、新車販売台数（同）も増加した。サービス消費では、宿泊の JCB 消費額（同）
と外食の JCB 消費額（同）は天候不順もあっていずれも前月から減少した。一方、新幹線輸送
量の前年比伸び率は前月から拡大した。

＜先行き＞個人消費は底堅く推移する見込み／価格上昇の広がりに注意 
先行きの個人消費は底堅く推移するとみている。名目賃金の伸びが高水準で推移する一方、
政府の物価高対策による物価の押し下げ効果の剝落などで、物価上昇率が高まり、実質賃金が
伸び悩む可能性がある。 
名目賃金の伸びは高水準で推移するだろう。春闘の結果5は前年をやや下回るものの、高水準
となった。また、 2026 年度の最低賃金の 引き上げ率は全国加重平均で＋ 5.0％（前年度は＋
6.3％）と高水準を維持した6。 
消費者物価について、7 月使用分から 9 月使用分まで実施される電気・ガス料金の補助とい
った政府の物価高対策が終了した。加えて、代替調達もあって原油の輸入価格は高止まりして
おり7、原材料費や輸送費の上昇を通じて幅広い分野の価格に波及し、消費を下押しする可能性
には注意が必要だ。帝国データバンクによると、値上げされた飲食料品のうち中東情勢の影響
を受けた品目の割合は 9 月末時点で約 3 割だった。今後も値上げが継続する見込みだが、値上
げの品目数は10月で一旦ピークアウトする可能性もある8。 
また、金利上昇による住宅ローン等の利払い負担増が個人消費にもたらす悪影響にも警戒す
る必要がある9。さらに、消費動向調査（内閣府）によると、9 月の消費者態度指数（二人以上
の世帯、季節調整値 ）は 35.4（前月差▲0.1pt）と小幅に低下し、依然として低水準にあり
（巻末図表「消費者マインド」参照） 、節約志向の高まりに注意が必要だ。

4 詳細は、拙稿「消費データブック（2026/10/6 号）」（大和総研レポート、2026 年10月 6 日）を参照。 
5 日本労働組合総連合会（連合）「3 年連続の 5％超え！～2026 春季生活闘争 第7 回（最終）回答集計結果
について～」（2026 年7 月3日）によると、定昇相当込みの賃上げ率の平均は 5.01％（前年同時期は
5.25％）となった。 
6 厚生労働省「地域別最低賃金の全国一覧」（2026 年10 月9 日閲覧） 
7 世界的な需給の引き締まりも受け、ホルムズ海峡を経由しないルートでの中東や他地域からの原油の代替調
達の価格が割高となり、調達コストが押し上げられている。詳細は、神田慶司・畑中宏仁・中村華奈子・横田
凱「日本経済見通し：2026 年8 月」（大和総研レポート、2026 年8 月 24 日）を参照。 
8 帝国データバンク「『食品主要 195 社』価格改定動向調査 ― 2026 年 10 月 飲食料品値上げ、10 月は3153
品目 値上げラッシュはピーク越え」（2026 年9 月30 日） 
9 詳細は、神田慶司・畑中宏仁・中村華奈子・横田凱「日本経済見通し：2026 年8 月」（大和総研レポート、
2026 年8 月24 日）を参照。

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 歐洲央行上周四(9/10)為年內第二次上調利率，以應對能源價格上漲帶來的通膨
壓力 ， 並警告物價壓力可能比先前預期更久 ， 推動市場加大對後續進一步收緊政
策的押注 。 歐洲央行總裁拉加德形容此次决定爲”無需多想的决定”， 並警告通膨
回落至 2%目標的時間點目前預計在 2027 年底。歐洲央行預期明年平均通膨爲
2.5%，高於 6 月的 2.3%；2027 年核心通膨預期爲 2.6%，高於此前的 2.5%。
⚫ 軍事消息人士稱，與伊朗結盟的胡塞武裝上周四(9/10)奪取葉門港口城市的控制
權 ， 並沿紅海海岸向南推進至戰略島嶼地區 。 美國總統川普表示 ， 他預計伊朗戰
爭將在美國中期選舉後結束。若胡塞武裝控制這條與 荷姆茲海峽同樣重要的水
道 ， 其支持者伊朗將在與美國的戰爭中獲得關鍵戰略優勢 ， 並進一步推高油價 。
⚫ 美國勞工部勞工統計局上週四(9/10)公布 8 月生産者物價指數 PPI 年增 5.4%，

高於市場預期的 5.3％。週五(9/11)公布 8 月消費者物價指數(CPI)年增 3.4%與前
月相同，核心 CPI 年增率 2.4%符合市場預期，月增率達 0.3%高於市場預期。
FED WATCH顯示數據公布後推升市場對於聯準會本周將升息的預期。
⚫ 日本央行數據顯示，8 月國內企業物價指數較去年同期上漲 7.6%。增幅高於市
場預估中值 7.4%。7 月漲幅經修正後爲 7.7%；8 月進口物價指數同比上漲 24.8%，
7 月修正後爲上漲 29.3%， 表明當月日圓貶值以及中東局勢影響推高進口成本和
整體通膨，同時鞏固市場對日本央行在 9 月會議進一步升息預期。

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
美元兌新台幣匯率走勢
Sep/14/2026
＜上週走勢＞
上週台幣匯價高點為 31.455，低點在 31.698，以 31.638 作收，較前一週收盤價
貶值 0.8 分。
上週台幣先升後貶，週初，受到市場對於日本央行 9 月升息的預期升溫，日圓
走升帶動美元指數跌破 99 整數價位低檔震盪 ， 加以台股高檔震盪 ， 外資延續匯入的
走勢，帶動台幣升破31.500 至 31.455 週內高點；臨近週末，美國公布8 月生產者物
價指數年增率由上個月的 4.8%上升至 5.4%，數據公布後，FEDWATCH顯示聯準會
9 月升息機率攀升至 7 成，美元指數回到 99 上方，台股高點回落，外資偏向匯出，
台幣由升轉貶至 31.698 週內低點，終場在央行調節之下收在 31.638。
＜未來分析＞
預估本週台幣區間震盪。國際市場方面，上週五公布美國 8 月核心消費者物價
指數月增率 0.3%高於市場預期的 0.2%，數據公布後，提高本週聯準會升息的機率

至接近 9 成，此外中東局勢仍有升溫風險，油價持續高檔震盪，美元指數在 99 整數
關口盤整；就台灣市場來看，台灣 8 月出口衝上 824 億美元，創下歷年單月新高紀
錄，月增 9.4%，年增 41%，展現極為強勁的成長動能。顯示受惠於 AI 需求全面爆
發，帶動外銷出口暢旺，基本面強勁持續支撐台幣的表現；盤勢上，匯價逢低吸引
進口商及政府基金買盤進場，逢高則面臨出口商賣美金，且本週台美均有利率決策
會議，預估匯率波動將加劇。綜合以上，預估本週台幣走勢區間震盪。
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.400-31.900

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Sep/14/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週人民幣震盪走升。週初，雖然美伊戰火未平，帶動油價及美債利率高漲，
但市場對於日本央行 9 月升息預期升溫，日圓偏升帶動美元指數跌破 99 價位後低
檔震盪，人民幣延續偏升走勢，走升至6.7022 週內高點；臨近週末，美國公布8 月
生產者物價指數 5.4%優於市場預期 5.3%， 加以8 月核心消費者物價指數月增率 0.3%
優於市場預期的 0.2%，數據公布後，FEDWATCH顯示本週聯準會 9 月升息預期大
幅攀升至近 9 成，美元指數回到99 上方，人民幣回吐部分週初以來的漲幅，終場境
外人民幣收在 6.7071，週線升值 8 點，境內人民幣則收在6.7079，週線升值 29 點。  
＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣區間偏升。國際市場方面，美國通膨數據表現優於市場預期，
加以油價攀升至每桶 100 美元上方 ， 美債殖利率位居高檔 ， 美元指數也在99 上方盤
整，關注本週聯準會最新利率決議，預計在會議結果出爐以前，聯準會升息預期將
支撐美元指數，限制人民幣升值的空間。中國國內方面，人行中間價格緩步偏升，
上週升破 6.7800 價位，持續釋放人民幣偏強信號，支撐人民幣延續升值的方向。技
術面方面 ，USD/CNH 匯價居於各天期均線下方 ，且均線向下發散 ，加以匯價由低點
向上反彈測試 6.7150 關鍵價位後面臨壓力，顯示空方動能較盛，預計人民幣將延續
升值的態勢。綜合以上，預估本週人民幣走勢區間偏升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6800-6.7300

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Sep/14/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 USD/JPY 震盪下跌 。週初 ，因市場預期日本央行將加快升息步伐及資金將
回流日本，使 USD/JPY 跌破至 155 整數關卡之下；週間，USD/JPY 延續跌勢最低
來到 152.87 後反彈，後續大致在 153 至 154 間波動；臨近週末，美國公布 8 月 PPI
及 CPI 數據顯示通膨仍處高位 ，USD/JPY 一度反彈至154.6 後回落 ， 終場收在153.54，
週線大跌 270 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、上週公布日本第二季 GDP
較上季增長 0.4%，季增年率也由 1.1%上升至 1.4%，主因為企業資本支出改善，而
7 月名目薪資年增 4.7%為 30 年來最大漲幅，而實質薪資年增 2.4%也為 5 年多來最
大漲幅，顯示日本經濟復甦力道較原先預期強勁，進一步強化市場對於日央升息的
預期，有利日圓表現。二、美國 8 月 CPI 及 PPI 數據顯示美國通膨仍處高位，強化
市場對聯準會升息預期，且中東局勢升溫，帶動油價突破 100 美元，預期將支撐美
元，限縮 USD/JPY 下行空間。三、就技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY 匯價多次向
下測試 153 整數關卡未能有效突破，顯示下方有買盤支撐，而 155 關卡附近則為前
波壓力位，因此短線先以區間看待。綜合以上，預估USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震盪的
可能性較高。

美元兌歐元匯率走勢

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 EUR/USD 小幅下跌。 週初 ，EUR/USD 大致在 1.1600 至 1.1635 窄幅波動；
週間，市場等待歐洲央行利率決議及 美國通膨數據，EUR/USD 持續在 1.1615 至
1.1653 波動；週四晚間公布歐洲央行利率決議如預期升息 1 碼，而美國 8 月 PPI 高
於預期使市場對聯準會本月升息預期上升，EUR/USD 下行跌破 1.16 整數關口；臨
近週末，美國公布 8 月 CPI 顯示通膨仍具韌性，帶動 EUR/USD 走低來至 1.1568，
終場收在 1.1598，週線下跌 15 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、上週歐洲央行公布利率決
議如預期升息一碼，總裁拉加德表示即使面對能源價格衝擊，歐洲經濟第二季依然
展現韌性，預期第三季仍會延續，但未來通膨仍面臨上行風險，偏鷹派言論使市場
對於歐洲央行未來持續升息的預期上升，將帶給歐元支撐。二、美國 8 月 PPI 月增
0.4%符合市場預期 ， 年增5.4%高於預期 ，而 8 月 CPI 月增 0.4%年增 3.4%符合預期 ，
顯示通膨仍維持高位，使市場對聯準會升息預期增加，將支撐美元，不利歐元匯價
上行。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 匯價雖一度突破 1.1630 年線但未
能站穩，顯示上方仍有賣壓，而下方1.1550 附近則有支撐，在有效突破前以區間震
盪看待。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD 本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。

1.92%。 
美伊衝突升溫，帶動國際油價持續走揚，加劇市場對通膨之疑慮，加上美國8
月非農就業人數大增、8 月 PPI 年增率高於市場預期，市場預估聯準會下週升
息機率升至七成，帶動美債利率大幅反彈；台灣央行將於下週召開理監事會
議 ， 在台灣經濟成長與通膨維持高位下 ， 台灣央行面臨緊縮壓力 ， 預期台債殖
利率面臨走升壓力 ， 預估5 年券區間為 1.83%~1.90%，10 年券區間為 1.93%~2%。

市場焦點
聯準會9月
升息一碼
• 聯邦基準利率升至 3.75%~4.00%，經濟預測部分上調2026-2028年GDP成長率、失業率皆下
調，通膨降溫進程仍相對緩慢 ，點陣圖顯示今年還有升息一碼的機會 ，這次升息對股票而言 ，
聯準會沒有準備以大幅犧牲經濟與就業換取通膨下降 ，降低了硬著陸風險；對債券而言 ，短
端仍須消化更高的政策利率，但長端反而可能受惠於抗通膨可信度改善。
AI模型
開發放緩
• Anthropic、OpenAI及xAI相繼支持放緩最先進AI模型開發速度。不過，前沿模型開發速度放
緩，並不代表AI基礎建設需求同步轉弱。晶片、算力、資料中心及能源等AI基礎建設仍處於供
不應求格局 ，短期若因市場情緒或資金槓桿調整而出現回檔 ，較可能反映估值與持倉修正 ，

而非AI需求基本面反轉 。後續若前期累積的槓桿壓力逐步消化 ，且全球總體經濟及地緣政治
風險未進一步惡化，AI供應鏈仍具中長期配置價值。
台灣央行
• 利率決議重點： (1)台灣央行宣布基準利率維持不變 ，重貼現率、擔保放款融通利率和短期融
通利率分別維持2%、2.375%和4.25%。(2)放鬆房市信用管制 ，調整自然人第2戶購屋貸款最
高成數上限，由6成調升為7成。(3)上修今年經濟成長率及通膨率 ，GDP上修至11.48%、CPI
及核心CPI分別上修至2.03%及2.16。(4)央行預估明年AI基礎建設需求將持續貢獻台灣出口表
現，不過由於基期已墊高，預測明年台灣經濟成長率為5.82%。

貨幣決議與9月、7月聲明稿差異，不再強調能源衝擊 全員支持升息
• 聯準會9月升息一碼，聯邦基準利率在3.75%~4.00%。
聲明稿重點差異
紅色字體為9月的新增部分，括號內藍色字體為刪除的7月聲明措辭
FOMC以12票贊成升息一碼 批准發布以下聲明 ：委員會決定 ，將聯邦基金利率目標 區間上調至 3.75%至4%維持在
3.50%至3.75%，以支持聯準會的雙重使命。委員會延續其維持銀行體系充足準備金的政策。
儘管中東衝突在一定程度上帶來了高水準的不確定性 ，美國經濟活動持續以穩健步調擴張 。儘管地緣政治情勢 等因素
使經濟前景仍籠 罩高度不確定性，但國內支出保持韌性，生產力成長強勁，企業資本投資也相當強勁穩健。新增就業

人數大致跟上勞動力成長速度，失業率也變化不大，顯示就業市場目前仍大致維持穩定。
相較於委員會2%的目標，通貨膨脹率依然偏高 ，這在一定程度上反映了部分特定部門 (包括能源)因供給衝擊所導致的
價格上漲。今日的政策行動，將有助於讓通膨更及時地回到2%目標，委員會將實現價格穩定。

聯準會會後記者會問答重點整理
1
通膨「過高且持續太久」 當
前重心偏向物價穩定
通膨數據未改善，通膨過高且持續太久，聯準會當前的政策重心偏向雙重使命中的物價穩定 。透過政策
行動表明，聯準會對抑制通膨是認真的。
2
經濟、就業與地緣政治促成
一致升息
近幾週公布的數據證實美國經濟正在走強 ，基礎經濟成長率高於先前判斷；其次 ，勞動市場依然穩健；
第三，通膨持續高於目標，地緣政治局勢也改變經濟展望 。華許表示，這三項因素共同促成當天「堅定
且一致」的決定。
3
點狀圖暗示今年再升 1碼 但
不是政策承諾
最新經濟預測顯示，多數官員認為今年仍有進一步升息空間。12 名官員預期年底前再升息 1 碼，另有 4
人預期還會升息 2 碼，僅 2 人主張利率維持在目前水準。不過，點狀圖反映的是官員對適當利率路徑的

個別預測，並非正式的承諾。
4
拒絕提供前瞻指引 不預判下
次會議結果
面對下一次升息可能落在 10 月或 12 月的追問，華許表示「我不給出前瞻指引。」
他強調，先前公開表達的是對貨幣政策紀律的承諾 ，而不是對特定利率決策的承諾 。聯準會不會預判未
來任何會議結果，後續行動仍將依據最新經濟數據及展望作出決定。
5
升息非市場逼迫 迴避川普施
壓問題
面對此次升息是否由債市波動及市場壓力所推動的提問，華許予以否認。
他表示是根據就業走勢、經濟強度及整體情勢作出決定 ，市場價格可作為觀察依據，但不能決定聯準會
的政策。
資料來源：Fed，華爾街見聞，2026.9.17

台灣利率連10凍 明年經濟展望佳
• 利率決議重點： (1)台灣央行宣布基準利率維持不變 ，重貼現率、擔保放款融通利率和短期融通利率分別維持 2%、2.375%
和4.25%。(2)放鬆房市信用管制，調整自然人第2戶購屋貸款最高成數上限，由6成調升為7成。(3)上修今年經濟成長率及通
膨率，GDP上修至11.48%(六月預估9.45 %)、CPI及核心CPI分別上修至2.03%及2.16%(六月預估1.91%及1.90%)。
• 觀點：全球AI資本支出維持上調的趨勢，台股供應鏈訂單直接受惠，央行預估明年AI基礎建設需求將持續貢獻台灣出口表現，
不過由於基期已墊高，預測明年台灣經濟成長率為5.82%。
• 台股短線展望：上週隨著聯準會利率決議公布後，台股量能回升，顯示短線消息面或已利空出盡。權值股台積電領軍台灣加

權指數站上月線，技術指標偏正向。投資佈局方面，由於地緣政治、通膨及貿易關稅風險仍存在不確定性，建議AI概念持股
核心布局行業龍頭企業，留意中小型成長股較易受到外部因素而股價大幅波動。
資料來源：中央銀行，2026.9.17。f代表央行預測數。

美國零售強勁回升擴大內需支撐，惟價格壓力與升息提高市場波動
• 零售全面回升，消費動能展現廣度：美國8月零售銷售月增1.2%，創3月以來最大增幅，優於市場預期，並扭轉7月修正
後月減0.5%；非實體店面銷售月增 2.6%、電子及家電增 1.6%，餐飲服務亦增 1.2%，且扣除汽車與汽油後仍增 1.2%，
控制組更增1.4%，顯示消費回升並非僅由汽車與燃料支出帶動，第三季內需仍具支撐。
• 名目消費偏強，通膨與升息增添需求壓力： 不過8月零售銷售年增6.0%屬名目增幅，部分反映物價上升因素；同期 CPI
年增3.4%，汽油價格月增3.9%，能源成本上升可能壓縮家庭實質購買力 ，加上Fed於9月決議升息1碼，後續利率政策
仍將視通膨與經濟數據調整，使市場焦點由消費回升轉向評估高利率與高物價對後續需求動能的影響。

• 後市觀察：短期零售回升仍有助支撐美國景氣，但若能源成本持續走高，加上升息後借貸成本上升，消費動能能否維持
將成為重要觀察點；投資配置可偏向美國投資級短中天期債券 ，兼顧收益與利率風險，股票則可聚焦獲利穩定、具定價
能力的大型優質企業，降低需求放緩與資金成本升高的影響。

顧 • AI安全監管升溫，短期壓抑市場情緒：Anthropic、OpenAI及xAI相繼支持放緩最先進AI模型開發速度，並引入獨立第三方
評估等安全機制，引發市場對AI資本支出降溫的疑慮。不過，前沿模型開發速度放緩，並不代表AI基礎建設需求同步轉弱。
Alphabet、微軟及亞馬遜近期AI相關積壓訂單持續攀升，合計達約1.64兆美元，其中微軟及Alphabet分別突破6,000億及
5,000億美元，搭配雲端業務持續成長，顯示企業AI需求仍具韌性，相關訂單有望逐步轉化為未來數年的營收與獲利。
• 槓桿壓力逐步消化，AI供應鏈仍具中長期配置價值：美國雲端巨頭擴大AI資本支出，壓抑短期自由現金流；相較之下，亞洲
科技企業自由現金流可望由目前約2,450億增至2028年的6,330億美元，支撐亞洲科技供應鏈中長期發展。短期若因市場情

緒或資金槓桿調整而出現回檔，較可能反映估值與持倉修正，而非AI需求基本面反轉。後續若前期累積的槓桿壓力逐步消化，
且全球總體經濟及地緣政治風險未進一步惡化，AI供應鏈仍具中長期配置價值。

油價在美國中期選舉前，或將維持在高檔
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.14
伊朗戰爭期間卡達油氣收入暴跌
卡達政府的石油和天然氣收入 美伊衝突
• 美伊衝突短期內仍難見明確解方，彭博預估雙方後續可能採取的行動，主要可分為以下四種情境：
(1)伊朗承受經濟封鎖：伊朗承受美國經濟制裁與施壓，避免進一步重大軍事升級。在美方護航行動支持下，商船仍可維持通行
荷姆茲海峽，國際原油供應未出現明顯中斷，油價預估在每桶80美元區間波動。
(2)伊朗將攻擊範圍限於海灣地區油輪與美軍：伊朗主要鎖定海灣地區能源運輸船隻及美軍目標，美方則採取有限度軍事回應，

衝突維持在一定範圍內，油價預估在每桶90美元區間波動。
(3)伊朗擴大攻擊範圍：攻擊目標不再局限於海灣地區船隻及美軍基地，海灣能源基礎設施以及紅海等重要航運咽喉，均可能成
為目標。局勢升級期間，油價可能維持在每桶100美元以上，待衝突緩和後回落至70至80美元區間，此情境發生的可能性最高。
(4)美伊雙方全面擴大開戰：伊朗進一步擴大攻擊目標，導致美國軍事資產及區域能源基礎設施遭受重大破壞，甚至不排除以色
列重新加入戰局，油價可能升至每桶120美元。

全球債市拋售勢頭不墜，AI融資競逐資金推升美債殖利率
• 長端殖利率走高，財政與通膨壓力仍是主因 ：近期美國長
天期公債殖利率持續走高，其中10年期美債殖利率更突破
5%，創2007年以來高點；除能源價格上升推升通膨疑慮
外，美國財政赤字、政府債務及持續性的發債需求 ，也提
高市場對長天期公債供給與期限溢酬的要求 ，反映長端利
率壓力仍未完全紓解。
• AI企業擴大融資，增加公司債供給與資金競逐： AI資料中
心、晶片與電力基礎建設的資本支出快速增加 ，帶動大型
科技企業提高債券融資規模；截至 6月，華爾街市場策略
研究機構Yardeni Research在追蹤外國私人投資人過去一
年買進美國公司債約3,900億美元，高於美國公債約3,290
億美元，顯示資金配置確有轉向公司債的現象 ，不過市場

研究亦指出，AI企業發債對美債殖利率的直接推升效果相
對有限，應可視為推動長天期利率上行的眾多因素之一。
• 後市觀察：若財政融資與AI基礎建設支出持續維持高檔 ，
長天期公債仍面臨較大的供給與資金配置壓力；但 AI投資
仍具中長期成長動能 ，配置上可關注資料中心 、半導體及
電力基礎建設供應鏈 ，固定收益則以短中天期美債及高品
質投資級公司債為主，降低長天期利率波動的影響。

新興市場本幣債券擁高票息提供防禦力
• 高票息提供防禦力：對債券投資人而言，票息就像安全墊。當市場利率上升、債券價格下跌時，持續收到的票息可以部分甚
至完全抵銷價格損失。新興市場本幣政府債券今年的表現，比市場原先預期更具韌性。目前新興市場本幣債券的持有收益約
為4.54%，已經足以對利率風險形成相當程度的保護 ，即使今年存續期間對多數市場造成負面影響 ，票息收益仍讓整體報酬
維持正數。這也是為什麼新興市場本幣債券在美伊衝突爆發後一度重挫超過 6%，但之後仍能逐步反彈 。哥倫比亞、巴西、
匈牙利及墨西哥的債券表現尤其突出。
• 市場已反映相當程度的升息風險：問題在於未來幾個月投資環境可能比上半年更加困難，能源價格上漲推升通膨，也迫使新
興市場央行維持較為鷹派的貨幣政策 。市場目前甚至預期 ，19個主要新興市場中 ，有18個市場的債券殖利率可能在年底前

上升。殖利率上升意味著債券價格承壓，因此投資人不能只看表面的高票息，必須同時評估未來利率變化可能造成資本損失。

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.15

顧
新興市場本幣債券目前最大風險在於利率與匯率
• 存續期間仍是今年最主要的拖累因素：一般而言，債券的存續期間越高，當殖利率上升時，債券價格下跌的幅度就可能越大。
今年19個符合基準指數條件的新興市場中 ，有14個市場的總報酬受到存續期間拖累 ，其中韓國、馬來西亞、印尼、泰國及
菲律賓受到的負面影響最為明顯。更值得注意的是，新興市場債券的利率表現與美國公債高度相關，因此，投資新興市場債
券，不能只觀察當地央行，也必須密切留意美國聯準會利率政策及美國公債殖利率走勢。
• 匯率可能成為下一個重要變數：新興市場本幣債券除了債券價格本身，還存在另一項重要風險：匯率。今年即期匯率變動反
而對新興市場本幣債券帶來正面貢獻 ，約增加57個基點。但如果能源價格持續上漲並形成滯脹環境 ，順周期貨幣可能面臨

更大的貶值壓力 。目前市場預期年底前新興市場貨幣整體仍偏弱 ，19種新興市場貨幣中 ，有11種預計兌美元貶值 。其中土
耳其、哥倫比亞及巴西貨幣的潛在跌幅相對較大。這代表即使債券本身提供不錯的票息，如果當地貨幣大幅貶值，換算成美
元或其他投資人本國貨幣後，仍可能侵蝕甚至吞噬債券收益。
• 小結與投資建議：目前新興市場本幣債券比較像是高票息換取較高波動的投資，而不是單純追求資本利得的交易。在殖利率
已處於相對高檔、但利率與匯率風險仍未完全解除的情況下，採取分散市場、控制部位、逢回布局的策略會比追高更為合適。
新興市場本幣債券存續期間的貢獻度
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.15

• 多重資產配置可有效風險管理：美伊衝突持續及聯準會利率政策，使今年金融市場震盪加劇，加上第四季美國期中選舉到來，
增加市場更多不確定性，不過從歷史經驗來看，地緣政治事件對股市的影響多屬短期現象，市場在初期情緒性下跌後，通常會
逐步回歸基本面，相較於預測事件發展，投資人更應透過多元資產配置分散風險，以提升投資組合的穩定性與長期抗震能力。
• 透過多重資產廣納收益來源：在利率維持相對高檔的環境下，投資人更應重視收益來源的多元化，從下圖可見，相較於過去幾
年，掩護性買權、非投資等級債，及投資等級債等資產的收益率均顯著提升，提供投資組合更具吸引力的現金流來源，其中掩
護性買權收益率已超過10%，非投資等級債，及投資等級債亦分別達7%及5%以上水準，透過結合不同收益型資產，不僅有助

提升整體收益潛力，更能降低對單一市場或利率變動的依賴，在市場波動加劇時提高投資組合的穩定度與韌性，兼顧收益與長
期成長目標。
布局全球多重資產，掌握成長契機

兆豐銀行所做任何投資意見與市場分析結果，係依據資料製作當時情況進行判斷
惟可能因市場變化而變動，投資標的之價格與收益將隨時變動，亦不必然為未來績效表現 18
1. 本資料內容僅供內部同仁及客戶參考，非作為任何金融商品或服務之購買或銷售之推介，不得視為買賣有價證券或其他金融商品之要約或要約之引誘。
2. 本資料提及之經濟走勢預測不必然代表基金之績效，基金投資風險請詳閱基金公開說明書。
3. 基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益 ，投資人申購
前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 本資料所載內容如涉及商品資訊 ，商品相關資訊仍以經核可之條款及文宣 (DM)為主，本行網站已備有公開說明書可供查閱基金應負擔之費用及相關投資風險 。投
資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。
5. 本資料所載內容取自本公司相信可靠之資料來源，且為特定日期之判斷，本公司不保證其即時性、精確性及完整性，亦不提供或嘗試遊說客戶做為投資之判斷依據。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本
公司及本公司所屬員工，均不負任何損害賠償及其他法律上之責任。
6. 本資料內容之著作權屬兆豐銀行所有，禁止就所載資料任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。
7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重 。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良
管理人之注意義務外不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。
11. 屬基金資訊觀測站ESG基金專區所列之基金，有關基金之ESG資訊，投資人應於申購前詳閱基金公開說明書或投資人須知所載之基金所有特色或目標等資訊。

## Document 122: 261014_daiwa_ ITEQ

Source PDF: all report pdf/261014_daiwa_ ITEQ.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-14
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We preview ITEQ’s 3Q26 results (due on 6 November). In our 
previous note, “Upgrading: strong beat in 2Q26 with improving outlook” (7 
August), we had underestimated the magnitude and durability of price hikes 
for E-glass and M4-and-below CCL. We raise our 2026-28E earnings by 
43-70% to reflect better-than-expected pricing and gross margin 
performance, which leads to our higher TP of TWD1,110 (from TWD600).

What’s the impact: Ongoing price hike of E-glass and M4-and-below 
CCL. This week, a major Japanese copper-clad laminate (CCL) vendor 
raised its entry-level CCL price by 200% to catch up with other global 
peers’ price hike level. One of the Chinese peers is also having a constant 
c.10% ASP hike on a monthly basis in 2026. We are increasingly confident 
in the management’s target for its blended price hike in 2H26. We now 
forecast c.30%/20% price hikes in 3Q26E and 4Q26E, respectively (vs. our 
previous estimates of 20%/10%). Looking ahead, we expect another 10% 
price hike per quarter in 1H27 (c.20% price hike in total), while pricing 
should gradually stabilise in 2H27 given the rising supply of E-glass, in our 
view. While the visibility towards 2H27 and 2028 remains relatively limited, 
we believe the key upside risks to our current forecast will be the stronger-
than-expected price hike magnitude and durability of the current up-cycle.

3Q26 results preview. After factoring in stronger-than-expected price 
hikes, ITEQ’s 3Q26 revenue grew c.35% QoQ, mainly driven by the 30% 
price hike despite utilisation rate staying at c.75%. We lift our gross margin 
forecast to c.30.3% (from 24.7% in our August note), in line with the August 
preliminary results’ pre-tax margin of 25.8%. With price hikes continuing, 
we expect the 4Q26E gross margin to expand to c.35%. Given disciplined 
capacity expansion through 2028, we expect the higher GM to be sustained 
through 2028. Beyond price hike, we believe the key longer-term growth 
drivers include: a) rising regular server demand and new platform adoption 
(using M7 grade CCL) in 2027; and b) M9 certification by more clients.

What we recommend: We raise our 2026-28E EPS by 43-70% to factor in 
the price hikes. We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating, and raise our 12M TP to 
TWD1,110 (from TWD600), based on an unchanged target PER of 22x 
(higher than the range during the past 12 months of 5-18x) applied to our 1-
year-forward EPS. While we expect the current upcycle will eventually ease 
starting from mid-2027, we remain positive on the near-term price outlook. 
Key downside risk: weaker-than-expected regular server demand.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 36-60% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, mainly due to our more aggressive view on ITEQ’s price hikes 
and margin profile.

14 October 2026

Company profile 
ITEQ Corporation is a leading manufacturer of high performance copper clad laminate materials used 
for the fabrication of printed circuit boards and was founded in April of 1997. ITEQ’s products find use in 
many different application areas, such as computational and communications applications, Radio 
Frequency and Microwave devices, Automotive applications, and High Density Interconnect solutions.

New capacity announcements do not overturn our bullish view.
AI demand and increasing substrate complexity should keep the
market undersupplied from 2027, supporting further pricing and
margin expansion. We raise earnings estimates and price targets
for Unimicron and NYPCB and reiterate OW on both.
More capacity, but limited near-term relief: Suppliers are stepping up investment,

but several newly announced projects are not scheduled to ramp until late 2028-30
or beyond. Our updated supply-demand model continues to indicate undersupply
from 2027 through 2030, with tighter supply in CY27-28, which should only be
more favorable to pricing. Therefore, capacity additions announced in the past few
months do not, in our view, diminish the magnitude of pricing gains over the next
two years.
CY27 pricing should accelerate, amid tighter supply-demand in CY27-28, but

visibility on the magnitude remains limited: We see ABF substrate prices rising
25%+ in CY27, below buy-side expectations of ~50%. Our supply/demand outlook
points to undersupply next year, with larger substrates, higher layer counts, and
greater manufacturing complexity further constraining usable output. Raw material
cost pass-through was a key driver of CY26 price increases, we expect supply
tightness to play a greater role in CY27. This should allow pricing to outpace cost

inflation, driving faster margin expansion. We remain constructive on pricing, but the
magnitude and timing of increases depends on customer-level negotiations.
With pricing a segment-wide tailwind, we prefer Unimicron for its stronger CY27
capacity growth: As supply tightens, the ability to deliver incremental volume
should become a key differentiator. We favor suppliers bringing capacity online in
CY27 over those whose expansion plans contribute in later years. Unimicron’s

Guangfu II ramp should add 10-15% to capacity in CY27, compared with 5-10%
growth at NYPCB through debottlenecking, giving Unimicron greater scope to
benefit from both pricing and volume growth. Unimicron is also our preferred play
heading into earnings, as more demanding expectations for NYPCB leave less room
for an upside surprise. But we remain OW on NYPCB given its networking and AMD
CPU exposure, alongside its proactive pricing strategy.
We reiterate Overweight on Unimicron and NYPCB: Reflecting stronger earnings

trajectories, we raise our EPS estimates for both companies. Based on upside to our
revised price targets, our order of preference is Unimicron (NT$1,775 PT; 24x CY28e
P/E; 41% upside), followed by NYPCB (NT$2,000 PT; 22x CY28e P/E; 39% upside).
Potential risks: 1) Weaker demand for PCs, general servers, and AI servers; 2)
meaningful capacity expansion plans; 3) T-glass shortage; 4) CoWoP displacing ABF
substrates.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
October 14, 2026 08:00 AM GMT

2
Supply/Demand Update: Undersupply Deepens in CY27–28
Our updated model points to a wider supply shortfall in CY27–28, despite additional
capacity: Incorporating the latest expansion plans and semiconductor demand forecasts,
we now expect ABF substrate demand to exceed available supply by 9.3% in CY27 and
13.7% in CY28, versus 6.2% and 9.4% previously. This represents a widening of 3.1ppt and
4.3ppt, respectively, relative to our prior estimates. We maintain our view that CY26 is a

broadly balanced market, followed by increasingly tight supply over the next two years.
The main demand upgrade comes from other ASICs, including Chinese AI chips: We
raise our shipment assumptions for this category to 6.4mn units in CY27 and 8.7mn in
CY28, from 4.6mn and 6.2mn previously. Our Nvidia GPU and server CPU forecasts remain
largely unchanged, while we lower AMD GPU estimates while also keeping CY27 Trainium
and TPU shipments largely unchanged. The tighter substrate outlook therefore reflects

stronger demand across a broader set of ASIC suppliers, rather than higher shipment
assumptions for every major AI platform. We also raise our substrate content assumptions
for select chips based on our latest supply chain checks, lifting our substrate demand
estimates across some segments, including server CPUs and networking.
Higher supply assumptions provide limited relief in CY27-28: We now include additional
capacity announcements that have been announced recently, including two additional

Kinsus factories, scheduled to come online in CY28 and CY29, beyond the CY27 expansion
already in our model. Several larger projects, however, contribute later. NYPCB’s new site
starts ramping in 2H29, while SEMCO’s Sejong expansion begins mass production in
September 2028 and its project in Vietnam is scheduled to begin operations by June
2030. These additions increase medium-term supply but do not prevent the modeled
shortfall from worsening through CY28.

Increasing substrate complexity adds to the pressure on usable output: Larger body
sizes, higher layer counts, and more demanding manufacturing processes constrain the
output available to meet demand, even as suppliers expand installed capacity. With the
supply gap widening in CY27-28, we expect tightness to become a more important pricing
driver than raw material cost pass-through. This supports our forecast for 25%+ price
increases in CY27 and further increases in CY28, alongside continued margin expansion.

Supply-side developments
Unimicron (3037.TW, OW, covered by Howard Kao)
On September 30, Unimicron announced that it has acquired a Hukou site from
Bridgestone Taiwan for ~NT$10.089bn (~US$318mn). Based on our checks, this is for
capacity expansion plans in 2030 or beyond.
For CY26, Unimicron has announced capex of NT$53.7B.

Over the next few years, Unimicron already has several new production facilities coming
online, including:
•  Guangfu II: ramp in 2027
• Yangmei II: ramp in 2028
• Yangmei III: ramp in 2029
• Yangmei IV: ramp in 2029+
• Bridgestone: ramp in 2030+

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
NYPCB (8046.TW, OW, covered by Howard Kao)
On August 6, NYPCB announced that its Board of Directors had approved a capex budget
of NT$46.8bn (~US$1,474mn) to invest in building a new ABF substrate production site.
Our checks indicate that this production site is located in the southern part of Taiwan, and
will start to ramp up production in 2H29. Given the size of this investment, we estimate
that it will approximately double its capacity exiting CY30, relative to its CY28 capacity.

Kinsus (3189.TW, Not Covered)
In our previous model, we had assumed only one factory (k6b) coming online in CY27,
increasing capacity ~30%. However, recent checks suggest that Kinsus intends to bring
two more factories online, one in CY28 (k6c) and one in CY29 (k6d). This implies that
capacity would increase another ~25% in CY28 and ~20% in CY29, bringing Kinsus' total
ABF substrate capacity to almost double exiting CY29, relative to its CY26 capacity.

Ibiden (4062.T, covered by Shoji Sato)
Back in February, Ibiden first announced that it will invest capex of ¥500bn in the
electronics field planned through F3/29, for ABF substrate expansion. Sato-san's latest
view is that Ibiden will need a new factory building to install the equipment and facilities
that will be purchased with the ¥500bn. As a result, Sato-san expects Ibiden during its 2Q
results briefing (October 30) to announce plans to make a large capital investment,

including for a factory building with a long lead time, and raise its medium-term earnings
forecast (report here).
SEMCO (009150.KS, covered by Shawn Kim)
On September 29, SEMCO announced investment of KRW4.27 trillion (~US$3,178mn) in
Sejong and KRW2.51 trillion (~US$1,488mn) in Vietnam, for AI server semiconductor
substrate capacity expansion.
• Sejong: Investment period from September 2026 to May 2028, with mass
production starting in September 2028. Largest single-product investment to date,

serving as key hub for mass production of high-value-added, highly demanding
products, proactively responding to the increasing demand for AI server substrates.
• Vietnam: The Vietnamese subsidiary aims to complete construction and begin
operations by June 2030. It will be operated as a strategic hub to respond to global
volume demand and enhance production efficiency through a stable supply
system.
With this investment, Samsung Electro-Mechanics plans to actively respond to the

growing demand for high-value-added semiconductor substrates in high-growth markets
such as AI servers, HPC, and automotive electronics. In particular, this investment is being
pursued based on medium- to long-term cooperation with global big tech and
semiconductor customers. The investment includes customer funding support and
medium- to long-term volume guarantees; thus, the company expects to strengthen the
competitiveness of its FCBGA business based on a stable demand foundation.

LG Innotek (011070.KS, covered by Ryan Kim)

### Page 5

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 5
According to news reports (link), LG Innotek signed a memorandum of understanding
(MOU) with Haiphong, Vietnam, for an investment to expand its semiconductor substrate
plant. Under the agreement, LG Innotek will push to build a semiconductor substrate
plant in Haiphong.
•  Construction will begin in July 2026 and is scheduled to be completed in May
2027.
• The site spans approximately 330,000 square meters (98,000 pyeong), equivalent

to 45 soccer fields.
• According to a news report, LG Innotek will invest approximately $1 billion, or
about 1.53 trillion won, for its Phase 1 expansion plan, which mainly includes RF-SiP
(radio frequency system-in-package) and FC-CSP (flip chip-chip scale package).
• ABF substrates will be part of the Phase 2 expansion plan, but no details or
investment size has been disclosed, yet.
According to a press release from LG Innotek on September 30, LG Innotek started mass

production of ABF substrates for PC chipsets targeting big tech clients last year. It
initiated high-volume mass production of FC-BGA for PC CPUs in the third quarter of this
year. Simultaneously, the company is rapidly boosting revenue by securing additional
major clients. Starting next year, it plans to sequentially enter the AI accelerator and AI
server markets.
• Revenue from LG Innotek's package solution business has maintained an upward
trajectory for three consecutive years. With the package solution business

demonstrating tangible results alongside its optical solution business, the
company has strengthened its capacity to generate stable earnings.
• LG Innotek plans to rapidly expand its market share in the emerging semiconductor
substrate market, aiming to build its package solution business, including
semiconductor substrates, into a business generating US$743 million in operating
profit by 2031.
• In the future, LG Innotek plans to develop its future businesses, including

semiconductor substrates and physical AI solutions, to US$5.94 billion by 2030.
The company aims to have such future new businesses account for 25% of total
revenue, thereby completing a well-balanced business portfolio.
The expanded plant will produce major semiconductor substrates including radio
frequency system-in-package (RF-SiP), flip chip chip scale package (FC-CSP), and flip chip
ball grid array (FC-BGA). "The Gumi site will serve as a 'mother factory' responsible for

developing new semiconductor substrate technologies and producing new models and
high-value-added products, while the expanded plant in Vietnam will be operated as a
production base for general-purpose semiconductor substrates," an LG Innotek official
explained.
AT&S (ATSV.VI, Not Covered)
On June 15, AT&S announced that it had signed key terms with AMD and Marvell to add
AI/HPC IC substrate capacity in Kulim, Malaysia. The company said the €1.5-2bn

investment (approximately US$1.68bn to US$2.24bn) is fully supported and financed by
long-term customer commitments, pending final execution.

Shinko (private)
On September 30, Shinko announced that it has entered into a purchase agreement with
Japan Display Inc. (JDI) to acquire its Mobara fab. In addition, it has entered into a sale
agreement with FICT Ltd. for the transfer of a portion of the fab to be acquired.
• Shinko will position the fab as a new core plant for advanced semiconductor
packages, and strengthen its production systems for flip-chip type packages
through the determination and implementation of capital investment plans for the

fab in conjunction with capacity expansion at its plants in the Nagano area.
Through these initiatives, Shinko aims to respond accurately to strong market
demand while pursuing sustainable business growth and enhancing corporate
value.
• The JDI Mobara fab has a total land area of 373,483 square meters, with Shinko
using the eastern area of the Fab (140,738 square meters, or ~38%) and FICT
planning to use the western area (232,745 square meters, or ~62%).

Toppan (7911.T, Not Covered)
According to news, Toppan broke ground in March 2024 on a new factory on Pesawat
Drive near Singapore's Jurong Lake District, built through its local unit Advanced Substrate
Technologies (AST), a JV with Broadcom. It makes FC-BGA substrates, which is Toppan's
term for them. It is Toppan's first FC-BGA plant outside Japan. The plant opening
ceremony took place on September 30, 2026.
• AST operates a facility of approximately 70,000 m² in the vicinity of Jurong Lake

District and employs more than 200 highly skilled engineers and technicians.
• Focus on high-demand, large-size, high-layer-count substrates
• While the investment size is not disclosed directly by the company, Nikkei sized
the investment at around ¥50 billion (~US$317mn).
Daeduck (353200.KS, Not Covered)
Daeduck announced on July 20, 2026, that it is investing ₩497 billion (~US$370M)
manufacturing facilities and related infrastructure for semiconductor products, running

through December 31, 2027.
ABF capex is stepping up, but announced investment still falls short of implied
demand. We estimate that ABF substrate makers have announced ~US$14.3bn of
investment year to date, materially above the segment’s aggregate annual capex in recent
years, although these commitments span multiple years. Our tally is not exhaustive. LG
Innotek has yet to disclose the size of its planned ABF capacity investment, while Shinko
has not quantified the additional spending required to expand capacity following its

announced acquisition of a JDI fab.
Our supply chain checks suggest that each US$1 of investment can support US$0.50–
0.60 of annual revenue at full ramp. On this basis, the US$14.3bn announced so far could
support US$7.2–8.6bn of incremental annual revenue. This compares with US$11.4bn of
incremental revenue implied by our industry forecast. We model the ABF substrate
market reaching US$20.9bn in 2030, up from US$9.5bn in 2026. Despite the step-up in

### Page 7

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 7
capex announcements, the investments we can currently quantify appear insufficient to
meet our demand forecast, pointing to a need for further capacity expansion over the
coming years.

Stronger ASIC demand from hyperscalers
We have updated our ASIC demand forecasts in our ABF model to align with the estimates

from our Semiconductors team. As shown in the exhibits below, our revised assumptions
point to a higher estimate for Amazon's Trainium processors in 2026, as well as marginally
lowered shipment estimates for Amazon's Trainium processors and Google's TPUs in 2027,
while the other  estimates  stay largely unchanged. These ASICs are characterized by large
die sizes and high layer-count package substrates, and thus we believe the slightly
lowered 2027 volume estimates do not derail our constructive outlook for the substrate

suppliers.

3Q preview: Morgan Stanley estimates vs. consensus
Unimicron
We expect a slightly stronger gross margin of 27.6%, ~30bps above the Street estimate,
reflecting a more favorable product mix and improving pricing environment. On operating

expenses, our estimate is roughly the same as consensus, resulting in an operating margin
of 19.4%, ~70bps above the Street estimate. However, our non-op income estimate of NT
$661mn is materially higher than the Street's estimate of NT$1,495mn in non-op loss. This
leads to our EPS estimate of NT$5.21 being ~37% higher than the Street.

The State of the Current Cycle
The ABF substrate market has gone through a few cycles over the past two
decades. We believe we are now still in the early innings of an up-cycle.
There was a prolonged down-cycle from 2012-18 as the PC TAM shrank amid the rise of
smartphones and tablets. During this tough period, companies were not able to generate
profits and had to compete on price to fill their idle capacity. As a result, during those

years, there was no or very limited capacity expansion, and capital expenditures by the
major ABF substrate suppliers were kept at a minimum.
Even as general server demand picked up, lifting utilization rates across the group going
into 2020, ABF substrate suppliers remained cautious on the longer-term demand outlook
and remained disciplined, hesitating to expand capacity. Hence, capex turned up just
slightly in 2019-20, increasing ~US$600mn vs. ~US$2.2bn on average per year in 2012-18.

This underinvestment led to sudden undersupply, as the shock from Covid-19 drove strong
work-from-home demand for PCs, as well as increased data usage and storage for general
computing driven by online entertainment. This ultimately led to a significant increase in
ABF substrate market value from 2020-22, reflected by the sudden surge in demand
along with meaningful pricing adjustments owing to the lack of supply.
During this 2020-22 period, much capacity expansion was set in motion as ABF substrate

suppliers and customers came to agree that necessary investments were needed for
future growth. Some subsidies were even given to these suppliers to facilitate this
capacity expansion. This is why capex jumped from ~US$2.9bn in 2020 to US$4.1bn in
2021 and US$5.5bn in 2022; even in 2023-24, capex remained elevated, as capacity
expansion plans continued.
Unfortunately, demand did not play out as expected. As the world came out of the
pandemic, demand for PCs and online entertainment fell sharply. This normalization of

demand, coupled with capacity expansion from practically every major ABF substrate
supplier, resulted in the ABF substrate market once again becoming oversupplied. This led
to a sharp decline in ABF substrate market value in 2023-24, as volumes normalized,
driving prices down to normalize against the new supply/demand dynamic.
Fortunately, cloud demand remained strong for general computing, and with AI demand
starting to pick up, the down-cycle this time around was much shorter (two years) vs. the

prior down-cycle (six years).
In 1H24, the ABF substrate market troughed and we saw an upward inflection since then,
but the pace of the recovery has been moderate and bifurcated. Midrange/low-end
markets such as PCs have shown moderate demand recovery, with high-end server and AI-
related end-demand recovering and growing at a faster pace. This is why we turned the
page on our bearish stance in 1H25, but did not turn more bullish until early 2026.

### Page 14

M
Idea
14
Periods Description
Period I (2010-2012): PC-centric
equilibrium
ABF substrate demand is primarily driven and dominated by CPU and GPU demand for desktops, notebooks,
discrete GPUs, with a meaningfully smaller exposure towards server CPUs.
Period II (2012-16): PC decline
and demand dilution
Global PC demand entered  a structural down-cycle as consumer behavior changed, with smartphones and tablets
absorbing more consumer time spent. This resulted in lower volumes and increased pricing competition as

suppliers fought to fill idle capacity.
Period III (2016-18): The quiet
inflection - servers and early
HPC
During these years, the PC market matured, while cloud computing started to take off. At the same time, server
CPU substrates quietly grew larger with more layers. We also started to see early accelerators being adopted in
high performance computing.
Period IV (2018-2022): WFH
demand driven structural
shortage
The Covid-19 pandemic drove massive work from home demand for consumer electronics and cloud computing.

This sudden surge in PC demand and increased time spent on gaming and online entertainment, bundled with a
continuous increase in substrate package size and layer count, resulted in a structural shortage, which also resulted
in longer lead times and raised prices. It was during this period (2021-22) that ABF suppliers globally started to
invest in new capacity.
Period V (2022-2024): Post-
pandemic correction and new
capacity arrival
As the world came out of the Covid-19 pandemic, WFH demand faded and  PC and cloud computing demand were

negatively affected. But at the same time, the new investments and capacity additions were just starting to slowly
come online, resulting in a drop in utilization rates and pricing.
Period VI (2024-2026):
Bifurcated recovery
PC demand normalized at the pre-Covid levels, with some replacement demand driven by Windows upgrade, while
the recovery and growth of AI and general server demand for cloud picks up at a meaningfully higher growth
trajectory.

Period VII (2026-30): AI diffusion Demand for AI proliferates from training to inference, driving ABF substrate market growth to re-accelerate over the
next five years after a two-year down-cycle following the Covid-19 pandemic. This drives increased demand for AI
computing, networking, and cloud computing, while demand for PCs remains soft.

Breaking down ABF substrate end demand
The end market mix for ABF substrates has shifted dramatically over the past decade. PCs

were once the dominant demand driver, accounting for approximately 70% of global ABF
substrate market value in 2015, supported primarily by PC CPUs and GPUs. Since then,
however, the industry's demand profile has undergone a structural transformation. We
estimate that PC-related applications represented only ~23% of total ABF substrate
market value in 2025, and this share is expected to decline further to below 10% by 2030.
In contrast, demand from servers, AI GPUs, AI ASICs, and networking applications has

expanded rapidly. We estimate that these segments collectively accounted for only
around 10% of the ABF substrate market in 2015, but their share increased to ~60% by
2025. Looking ahead, we expect they will represent more than 80% of total ABF
substrate market value by 2030.
In our view, this structural shift in end-market demand has been the primary driver of ABF
substrate industry growth over the past decade and will become even more pronounced

over the next five years. As AI infrastructure deployment accelerates, we expect continued
expansion in server, AI accelerator, and networking demand to drive meaningful
acceleration in ABF substrate market growth.

Price Target and Valuation Methodology
Our 12-month price target increases ~21% to NT$1,775 (from NT$1,465), driven by our
increased earnings estimates for CY26-28: We believe the company's earnings have
returned to a growth track from 2026 onward and will show stronger  growth in 2027,
driven by the high-end ABF substrate market's reversion to undersupply and Unimicron's
dominant supply share in AI ASICs and also server CPUs, which puts it in a favorable

position to benefit from rising AI investments.
We believe the overall ABF substrate market bottomed in 2H24, and see growth
accelerating from 2H26 onward with the ramp of multiple next-generation, large body size
ABF substrates for new server GPUs, CPUs, ASICs, and networking chips. New
technologies such as embedded passives and Intel's EMIB-T should also increase the
manufacturing complexity of the package substrates, which adds to the under-capacity as

usable output decreases.
Similar to our analysis of other tech hardware companies within our coverage, we use a
residual income (RI) valuation model to value Unimicron – we think it derives the most
accurate value of the firm as it takes into account cost of equity. Our key assumptions
include:
• Cost of equity of 9.2% (unchanged)
° Risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield);
° Equity risk premium of 8.7%;
° Beta of 1.0;
• Medium-term growth rate of 14% (slightly lowered from 15%)

• Terminal growth rate of 3% (unchanged, similar to the rest of the companies under
our tech hardware coverage).
Our bull and bear case values increase by similar magnitudes, to NT$3,030 and NT
$595, respectively.

Morgan Stanley Research 23
Price Target and Valuation Methodology
Our price target increases ~29%, to NT$2,000, reflecting our raised earnings
estimates for CY26-28: We believe the company's earnings have returned to a growth
track from 2026 onward and will show stronger growth in 2027, driven by the high-end
ABF substrate market's reversion to undersupply, NYPCB's dominant supply share for
networking chips, and its proactive pricing strategy, which puts it in a favorable position to

benefit from rising AI investments.
We believe the overall ABF substrate market bottomed in 2H24, and growth should
accelerate from 2H26 onward with the ramp of multiple next-generation, large body size
ABF substrates for new server GPUs, CPUs, ASICs and networking chips. New
technologies, such as embedded passives and Intel's EMIB-T, should also increase
manufacturing complexity of the package substrates, adding to the overall undercapacity
as usable output decreases.

Similar to our analysis of other tech hardware companies within our coverage, we use a
residual income (RI) valuation model to value NYPCB – we think it derives the most
accurate value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. Key assumptions
(all unchanged):
• Cost of equity of 9.3%;
° Risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield);
° Equity risk premium of 8.7%;
° Beta of 1.0;
• Medium-term growth rate of 17%  –  fair given our analysis of the substrate market

over the next couple of years;
• Terminal growth rate of 3% (similar to the rest of the companies under our tech
hardware coverage).
Our bull and bear case values increase by similar magnitudes, to NT$3,575 and NT
$1,020, respectively.

MS Rating
▪ We expect the ABF substrate market to
revert to undersupply from 2027 onward,
with the undersupply gap reaching 26% by
2030.
▪ This is primarily driven by AI demand, with
next-gen AI/networking chips adopting
larger body size ABF substrates.
▪ In the near to medium term, the short
supply of T-glass will be a key watch point,
but we believe this will mainly affect low-

end ABF and BT substrates, as suppliers
prioritize supply to the AI chip vendors.
▪ Our price target implies 41x 2027e P/E or
24x 2028e P/E, above the 10-20x range at
which it traded in the previous up-cycle. We
deem this fair, given AI chip substrate spec
upgrades and suppliers' ability to raise
prices.

2026-27, leading to weaker margins for
Unimicron over the next two years.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Takeaways on Photonics from
2026 OCP Global Summit
Morgan Stanley Asia Limited+
Andy Meng, CFA

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line

On Day 3 of the 2026 OCP Global Summit, there is a full-day special focus on
Photonics. Industry participants such as NVIDIA, Meta, Microsoft, Oracle, Broadcom,
Celestica, Arista, Ciena, Amphenol, Lumentum, and Huawei presented their views.
We summarize the key findings below:
1) When the moderator asks key industry players whether they will apply CPO or
NPO, almost all companies answered 'Both'.
2) AI density depends on optical connectivity. With the day's focus on Photonics, the

importance of optical connectivity was well highlighted in each session.
3) Five key metrics for new optical technology: 1) performance, 2) power, 3) cost, 4)
speed to deployment and 5) reliability and serviceability. Huawei believes NPO has
advantages on maintainability, open decoupling, availability and industry
implementation.
4) Scale-in is another new approach to enhance AI density.
5) Root cause of CPO failures: 83-85% of failures due to ELS, 11-17% due to fiber/

connector. Dust and dirt are also common failure issues.
6) Optics vs. Copper: Full Optical Pod is targeted towards scale-up sizes >576
accelerators, Optics and Copper to coexist between 72 and 576 accelerators.
Greater China Technology Hardware: Takeaways on NPO from 2026 OCP Global
Summit (14 Oct 2026)
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
A full day discussion of Photonics further demonstrated the importance of
optical connectivity.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  1

2026/10/19 投資速報

電機機械 
時碩工業 (4566.TT) 
液冷散熱驅動獲利躍升，2027 年成長動能延續

加權指數報酬率 2.8 3.3 26.9 70.4 
個股股價報酬率 27.6 24.1 17.0 46.8

營收走勢圖

研究員/張均壕 JUN_HAO1@uni-psg.com

研究部仍看好時碩未來營運，主要是考量液冷散熱零件業務隨著客
戶訂單逐步放量及出貨規模擴大 ，帶動 2H26 業績大幅提升。 此外 ，
航太事業成長前景仍佳，工業事業亦可望持續增長，明年展望依舊
正向。研究部預估時碩 2026 年 EPS 3.52 元(+162%YoY)，2027 年
EPS 4.63 元(+32%YoY)。以明年獲利為評價基礎，給予 20 倍本益
比，目標價 93 元。

重點摘要：

1. 受惠AI趨勢，液冷散熱訂單將帶動3Q26業績強勁增長： 
時碩3Q26合併營收上升至18.18億元(+24%QoQ，+43%YoY)，主
要是受惠AI資料中心液冷相關零組件的貢獻增加，令7、8、9
月單月營收持續創新高 。考量營收擴大， 規模效應顯現， 預期
時碩3Q26毛利率將提升至 24.5%，營益率提升至 8.8%，預估
3Q26稅後淨利1.38億元(+101%QoQ，+34%YoY)，預估3Q26EPS 
1.26元。

2. 液冷散熱產品進入爬坡期，4Q26營運淡季不淡： 
展望4Q26， 雖然年底通常為時碩汽車、工業業務的淡季， 但受
惠散熱零件仍處在出貨爬坡期，預期季度營收與獲利仍有望
再墊高。研究部預估時碩 4Q26合併營收18.85億元(+4%QoQ，
+55%YoY)，4Q26稅後淨利1.40億元(+1%QoQ，+113%YoY)， 預估
4Q26EPS 1.27元 。 全年度來看 ， 研究部預估時碩2026年合併營
收將提升至65.25億元(+31%YoY)，年度毛利率24.1%，營益率
7.1%，稅後淨利3.87億元(+161%YoY)，預估2026年EPS 3.52
元。

3. 航太業務維持成長動能，積極拓展新商機 
受惠於全球航太產業需求穩健及上游關鍵零組件產能吃緊，
時碩管理層持續看好航太業務前景 ，其中天陽航太營運成長
率預估可達15%～20%。 此外 ， 公司積極擴充泰國航太產能 ， 規
劃建置鋁合金及不鏽鋼生產線，以掌握全球航太供應鏈重組
及非紅供應鏈商機 ， 中長期目標為貢獻約10億元營收 。 除既有
航太業務外，時碩亦積極拓展人形機器人及半導體設備等新
興應用領域。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

在人形機器人方面， 時碩已投入諧波減速器及關節零組件開
發，並與國際及中國大陸主要廠商展開合作。半導體設備方
面，時碩持續開發相關精密零組件，未來將配合泰國新廠布
局 ， 爭取台灣客戶海外工廠的供應商機 。 整體而言 ， 隨著航太
業務穩健成長，以及人形機器人與半導體設備等新領域逐步
拓展 ， 將有助於公司建立更多元的營運成長動能 ， 降低對傳統
汽車產業的依賴。

4. 明年業績續升，預估2027年EPS 4.63元： 
展望2027年 ， 考量日系大廠的液冷散熱訂單將延續至明年 ， 而
且還有美系散熱大廠客戶訂單與磁浮壓縮機關鍵訂單等待發
酵 ， 加上航太業務仍具偏強增速 ， 預期時碩2027年業績將持續
提升。研究部預估時碩2027年合併營收74.20億元(+14%YoY)，
年度毛利率 25.0%， 預估2027年EPS將進一步提升至 4.63元。

主要競爭對手：

張智皓 chihhao.chang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

系統電 (5309 TT) 
2027 年重回成長軌道

永豐觀點 
系統電連三個月營收創下新高，預期在 BBU 及無人載具開始出貨下，營收逐月成
長。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持中立投資建議：系統電在經歷營運轉型整合期後，2027 年營收及獲利將重返
成長 ， 然研究員仍維持中立的投資建議 ， 主係考量(1) 營運槓桿效應須待 2H27 時，
BBU 放量出貨較明顯。此外，系統電為因應未來產能持續擴充 BBU 及無人機產線
侵蝕獲利；(2) 評價上，以 2027 年稀釋後稅後 EPS 2.17 元計算，目前本益比約
30 倍，評價尚屬合理，故維持中立的投資建議，持續關注 BBU 出貨及新訂單狀
況。

### Page 2

2

系統電營運主力將轉移至 BBU 相關應用： 系統電子工業股份有限公司成立於1977
年，原專注在車用電子、能源、通訊、電玩遊戲。近年公司陸續進行業務調整及轉
型 ， 將業務分為汽車電子 、 工業物聯網 、AI 資料中心能源系統管理三大事業 ，2015
年推出藍牙胎壓偵測器(BLE TPMS)， 目前主要經營業務內容為車用胎壓偵測系統 、
車用電子產品、綠色能源及儲能設備、電源供應器，並獲有多項專利，並持續擴大
BLE TPMS 應用在歐美 OE 前裝市場，如新能源訂動車、大客車車隊、皮卡等。近
期受到資料中心用電需求增加，系統電 BBU 相關產品將開始出貨。1H26 產品比
重：TPMS 46%、工業電腦 38%、AI 資料中心能源系統及其他 16%。 
3Q26 強固型工業電腦出貨旺，：系統電 9 月營收 3.67 億元(+12.04%MoM，
+17.87%YoY)，連續三個月創下歷史新高，主係因強固型電腦產品出貨。雖 BBU
及無人機相關產品尚未大量出貨，然在工業電腦領域，受惠產業趨勢帶動，如農耕
車及無人載具相關應用成長快速，使系統電 3Q26 營收 10.14 億元(+13%QoQ，
+24%YoY)，研究處預估毛利率 23.2%(+1ppt QoQ，+1.9ppt YoY)，因積極投入
研發及新產品設計使營業費用較高，稅後淨利 0.04 億元，稅後 EPS 為 0.02 元。 
4Q26 兩大專案 BBU 及無人機小量出貨 ：先前耕於已久的 BBU 產品將於十月開始
出貨 ， 初期以3kW 產品為主 ， 系統電已完成建置 2 條全自動 與 1 條半自動 BBU
產線。無人機方面，包含軍用及微型軍警偵察需求強勁，產線亦完成建置。研究處
預期 4Q26 營收 11.17 億元(+10.2%QoQ，+28%YoY)，毛利率 24%(+0.8ppt 
QoQ，-0.6ppt YoY)，稅後淨利 0.07 億元，稅後 EPS 為 0.03 元。預估 2026 年營
收 38.9 億元(+14.7%YoY)，毛利率 23.6%(+0.1pts YoY) ，稅後淨利 0.24 億元(-
54.3%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 0.11 元。 
2027 年迎來營運收成期：展望 2027 年，以三大事業體區分，工業電腦方面將聚
焦於邊緣算力模組、GCS 地面控制站，部門產品毛利率最佳，有利於獲利表現；AI
資料中心能源系統方面，BBU 產品將開始大量出貨，並持續擴充產能，預估部門
營收將年增 328%，並將增加 3-4 條 BBU 產線，因應未來新產品的加入；TPMS
業務收到車市影響，營運相對平淡。綜上所述，系統電2027 年營運將開始收成過
去在無人載具及 BBU 電源產品的投入，研究處預期 2027 年營收 61.68 億元
(+58.5%YoY)，毛利率 24.9%(+1.3pts YoY) ，稅後淨利 5.41 億元，稅後 EPS 為
2.36 元。

營運現況與分析

估 3Q26 稅後 12 億元，季增 15%，年增 27%，EPS 7.67 元 
3Q26進入旺季，營收季增 3%至 496億元，年增 48%，估毛利率季減 0.1
百分點至 19.2%，主因低毛利率子公司帆宣比重提高 0.3百分點及記憶體持
續上漲。估稅後 12億元，季增 15%，年增 27%，EPS 7.6 7元，比原估下
修 5%，主因營收低於原先預期 4%。4Q26 NCR代工訂單/帆宣美國二廠持
續增加，估營收季增 3%，毛利率季持平，稅後 14.3 億元，季增 19%，
EPS 9.1元，比原估上修 8%，主因 Kontron持股由 30%上升至 49%。 
提高 Kontron 持股，有助中長期投資效益 
樺漢提高德國子公司 Kontron AG 持股由原先 29.7%上升至 49.4%(8/20交
割完成 )。影響： 1)財務面：本中心 預期提高持股後， 估計可望提升獲利
10%以上。2)軟硬體與通訊互補：Kontron具有嵌入式運算、5G通訊、資安、
交通及國防航太技術，結合樺漢在邊緣 AI 伺服器、機器人及強固型工業電
腦的硬體優勢，從「單點設備銷售」轉型為「平台化解決方案」。 
上修 2026/27 年獲利 1%/6% 
智慧工廠與廠務(帆宣)目前在手訂單 1,578 億元，MoM+17%，未來成長趨
勢明確。展望 2026~27 年主要工程認列包括台積美國 P2~P3及 AP、高雄
廠 P3~P4、新竹 20廠 P3、台中 18廠 P9等，看好新廠商機，包括設備代
工、代理等，其中封裝設備估計 >3 成之成長。由於認列美國二廠業績加快，
估 2026/27年營收年增 55%/14%至 801/913億元，創新高。 
合計樺漢 2026/27 年營收 1,864/2,104 億元，成長 31%/13%，規模經濟向
上，費用率下滑，估營業利益率 5.57%/5.64%，優於 2025年 3.99%。稅後
44.4/53.6 億元，年增 38%/21%，EPS 2 8.25/34.14 元，比先前預期上修
1%/6%，主因升高子公司 Kontron持股。樺漢受惠提升 Kontron持股及帆宣
認列美國二廠增加，2026/27年營收及獲利高成長，維持買進評等。

估 3Q26 稅後 12 億元，季增 15%，年增 27%，EPS 7.6 7 元，比原估下修 5%，
主因營收低於原先預期 4% 
3Q26 進入旺季，NCR 代工訂單持續拉貨，子公司帆宣美國二廠進入認列高峰期，營收季增 3%至
496億元，年增 48%，估毛利率季減 0.1百分點至 19.2%，主因低毛利率子公司帆宣比重提高 0.3
百分點及記憶體持續上漲。估營業利益 28.3億元，季增 5%，年增 504%。業外方面，投資收入及
其他收益挹注下，共獲利 5.8億元。估稅後 12億元，季增 15%，年增 27%，EPS 7.67元(以最新
稀釋後股本 15.7 億元計算)，比原估下修 5%，主因營收低於原先預期 4%(智慧軟體方案部門需求
疲弱)。

記憶體漲價大部分轉嫁給客戶，略影響毛利率，但規模經濟向上，營業利益率上揚 
關於記憶體漲價因應，公司已與供應商鎖定全年約 200億元訂單所需之記憶體供貨量，價格雖隨市
場浮動，但會透過長約定價或調價等轉嫁給客戶，若記憶體價格漲幅過大也採客戶 consign 模式，
降低毛利率影響。1Q26 部分客戶接受轉嫁，部分客戶則選擇先觀望遞延專案，造成營收不如預期。
但 2Q26 後客戶已逐漸接受記憶體漲價，出貨恢復正常。由於記憶體轉嫁有落後且難以全數移轉，
加上近期低毛利率的子公司帆宣廠務成長較快，因此估 2026/27 年毛利率 19.4%/19.3%，略低於
2025年 20.5%。但預估 2026/27年整體營收成長 31%/13%，規模經濟明顯向上，費用率下滑，營
業利益率 5.57%/5.64%，遠優於 2025年 3.99%。

積極佈局實體 AI 
公司將實體 AI(Physical AI)定義為 AI由數位分析與內容生成，進一步具備感知、理解、決策及實際
執行能力。樺漢過去由 IPC 1.0 工業電腦、IPC 2.0 工業物聯網及 IPC 3.0 智慧平台逐步升級，
2026年開始進一步布局 Physical AI。 
公司建立完整機器人系統工程團隊，涵蓋 AI 感知與理解、機器學習系統、機器人運動控制、嵌入
式運算平台、數位孿生模擬及可靠度設計。 AI 感知包含影像、語音、感測器融合及 VLA（Vision-
Language-Action）技術；模擬驗證採用 NVIDIA Isaac Sim 進行虛擬場景、產線及機器人流程模擬
測試，用以最佳化生產效率。樺漢競爭策略由單純提供板卡與運算模組，轉向提供完整機器人系統
平台。產品布局涵蓋 Sense、Compute、Connect、Control及 Act五大機器人平台零件，應用包括
AMR自主移動機器人、人形機器人、工業製造、智慧物流及商業服務。  
運動控制及機器人相關技術仍在持續投資；模擬驗證軟體毛利率可超過 60%。管理層認為 Physical 
AI 初期仍以研發與平台建置為主，短期商業化重點為工業及商用 AI 應用，人形機器人獲利貢獻仍
需時間。2026年實體 AI為初期發展階段，貢獻樺漢業績約 3億美金，占營收比重 5.2%。長期實
體 AI 於製造場域可協助採購與物流管理、改善長短料問題，並透過即時感測與自動化控制提升工
站品質、精度及生產效率，研調機構預估全球實體 AI產值由 2025年 58億美金，上升至 2035元
產值可達 1200 億美元， CAGR 35.4%。樺漢結合既有智慧工廠與工業物聯網客戶基礎，推動
Physical AI走向實際部署，可望快速成長。

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧整理 
提高 Kontron 持股，有助中長期投資效益 
因樺漢集團對 Kontron的總持股比例已來到約 29.72%，接近德國證券法規定的 30%強制公開收購
門檻，樺漢董事會正式授權董事長啟動現金公開收購之前置作業(訂定每股 23.5歐元)。樺漢公開收
購並提高德國子公司 Kontron AG 持股由原先 29.7%上升至 49.4%(8/20交割完成)，核心效益與影
響主要體現在以下幾個面向： 
1)財務面：本中心預期提高持股後，估計可望提升獲利 10%以上(少數股權項目扣除減少)，雙方深
化整合後，透過樺漢母公司鴻海製造資源，預期從 2027 年開始全面發酵具體的營收與成本規模經
濟效益。

### Page 6

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026年 10月 19日 Ennoconn 第6 頁，共 19頁  
2)業務與技術整合(實體 AI 平台化) 
• 軟硬體與通訊互補：Kontron具有嵌入式運算、5G通訊、資安、交通及國防航太技術，結合樺漢
在邊緣 AI 伺服器、機器人及強固型工業電腦的硬體優勢，從「單點設備銷售」轉型為「平台化解
決方案」。 
• 加速跨場景落地：有助於將無人結帳、智慧零售、 AMR（自主移動機器人）及工廠自動化等應用
標準化，降低客戶導入門檻，並加快在北美及歐洲市場的開案與交付效率。  
Kontron 成長領域包括交通運輸、國防航太、 5G/FRMCS/NAD，以及軟體與解決方案，營收目標
由 2026年 16億歐元上升至 2030年 26億歐元(CAGR 13%)、EBITDA達 4.2億歐元，並藉由提高
平台及服務收入占比改善獲利結構。

結盟 NCR 增加 ODM 訂單 2Q26 開始出貨，有助工業物聯網部門成長 
樺漢與 NCR Voyix (股票代號：VYX US)結盟，該公司是全球消費者技術的領導者，主要生產 POS、
自動櫃員機、條碼閱讀器等，提供高品質的技術解決方案，並幫助企業在快速變化的數位環境中 蓬
勃發展。NCR Voyix 在市場領先的軟體和服務與樺漢的硬體專業知識技術高度互補，並有助樺漢
ESaaS及 ODM業務進一步擴展，為雙贏的結盟。 
NCR年營收約 1,230億元，其中硬體占比 32%，軟體 68%，硬體部分約 400億元台幣訂單未來全
交給樺漢代工，其中 160億元為新接訂單[NCR之前即為樺漢 ODM客戶，60%硬體(約 240億元)
交樺漢生產]，此部分將貢獻在工業物聯網 (含 Kontron、ODM)業務上，2025 年因應川普關稅議題，
NCR IT 全球性服務需調整期，發展整合系統；樺漢墨西哥製造基地調整至田納西。因此原本增加
NCR代工訂單由 10月再度延至 2026年 1月出貨，後因美伊戰爭及 DRAM漲價影響，客戶延至
2Q26正式出貨。受惠此訂單增加，估 2026/27 年工業物聯網營收成長 24%/13%至 845/958億元。

智慧工廠與廠務(帆宣)進入美國二廠密集認列期，營運創新高 
智慧工廠與廠務(帆宣)目前在手訂單 1,578億元，MoM+17%，主要來自晶圓代工廠、記憶體廠擴
廠積極，另外新增資料中心建置，半導體供應鏈普遍面臨交期、產能壓力，加上具有能力承接大型
專案廠商有限，使得廠務接近賣方市場，未來 2~3 年成長趨勢更為明確。展望 2026~27 年主要
工程認列包括台積美國 P2~P3及 AP、高雄廠 P3~P4、新竹 20廠 P3、台中 18廠 P9等，並且看
好新廠商機，包括設備代工、代理等，其中封裝設備估計 >3 成之成長。智慧工廠與廠務(帆宣) 認
列美國二廠業績加快，2026 年前 9 月營收成長 56%至 574 億元，第四季旺季營收持續向上，估
2026/27年營收年增 55%/14%至 801/913億元，創新高。 
2026/27 年年營收及獲利維持高成長 
智慧軟體方案策略進入 IPC 3.0Ⅱ階段，推廣工控/智慧平台，未來專注於雲端+邊緣 AI與大數據外，
亦提升工業級語言模型與機器人技術，聚焦產業包括：國防、航太、交通、零售、建築等。產品包
括：Edge AI Server & Device、Token、AI Agent、Physical AI等，公司積極佈局中，近期因專案
認列延後，估 2026 年營收 218 億元，衰退 3%。2027 年代理/實體 AI 發酵，估營收成長 7%至
233億元。 
合計樺漢 2026/27年營收 1,864/2,104億元(下修 2%/2%，主因智慧軟體方案營收低於預期)，成長
31%/13%，低毛利率的智慧工廠與廠務(帆宣)成長較大及上游原物料 Dram大漲，估 2026/27年毛

### Page 8

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026年 10月 19日 Ennoconn 第8 頁，共 19頁  
產業概況  
工業電腦未來 CAGR 6%，為下游成長穩定的產業 
工業電腦在 2000 年後網路發達，亦造成工業電腦用途日漸擴大，由工廠發展至生活端。過去常受
全球製造業景氣影響，後因應用面變廣，成長性加快。 
工業電腦近年成長率在 5~10%，是下游成長穩定的產業。根據研調機構統計及預估，工業電腦
2023年產值約為 45億美元，可望增加至 2037年的 96億美元，CAGR 6%比一般 PC低個位數成
長為佳，主因公司對工業物聯網、製造業數位化、邊緣 AI 建置…等因素推動，其中半導體、製造
業、汽車、物流、零售…等均為重要成長來源。 
圖 9：工業電腦 2023-2037 年產值 CAGR 6%

資料來源：Markets and Markets、元大投顧整理 
工業 4.0 帶動物聯網需求大增 
從 18世紀末經歷多次工業生產變革，20世紀末進入 21世紀，工業製造由 3.0發展至 4.0，其中最
大改變就是組裝、加工、搬運 …等由原先自動化機台 (工業 3.0)，加入智能回饋，透過物聯網收集
巨量資料，傳輸至雲端運算，統計分析後，即時改善生產製程。隨時因應機台老舊、材料或周遭環
境變化，自動調整量產系統。 
第四次工業革命和過去相比，網路無所不在，移動性提高；感測器體積變得更小、性能更強大、成
本也更低。與此同時，人工智慧和機器學習也開始嶄露鋒芒，相關應用不斷增加。

### Page 9

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026年 10月 19日 Ennoconn 第9 頁，共 19頁  
圖 10：歷次工業變革

資料來源：元大投顧整理 
預估人工智慧物聯網(AIoT)2025~2034 年 CAGR 達 22.16% 
物聯網基礎架構分為五層：物件、通訊與網路、運算與儲存、應用與服務、分析與統計。台灣工業
電腦公司較專注在前端物件 (感測元件)以及中端的運算與儲存二項業務。尤其前端的感測元件為台
灣強項，諸如：POS 機、ATM、機器人手臂、安控攝影機…等。近年受惠人工智慧(AI)導入，成長
加快。 
全球人工智慧物聯網(AIoT)未來受惠於 Data Center、AI Server 建置、企業數位轉型…等因素穩定
成長，研調機構預估 AIoT 2025~2034 年 CAGR 22.16%，為未來工業電腦成長重心。人工智慧物
聯網(AIoT)在全球使用廣泛，目前工業電腦產業使用方面已經陸續切入智慧家庭、車聯網、自動化
設備、機器人…等領域。

資料來源：網路、元大投顧整理

維持買進評等，目標價 545 元，潛在上漲空間 34.7%
與樺漢同樣從事工業電腦相關，在國內主要為研華、神基、飛捷、廣積、振樺電等。以 2027 年預

ODM 訂單開始出貨，加上子公司帆宣認列美國二廠進入高峰期，營收快速成長。另外樺漢增加
Kontron 持股，不僅提升獲利，亦有助長期代理/實體 AI 發展，因此維持買進評等。目標本益比同
先前 16倍，以 2027年 EPS 34.14元計算，目標價 545元，潛在上漲空間 34.7%。

公司簡介  
樺漢專精於工業電腦硬體解決方案。終端應用包括 POS機、銀行自動化、互動式資訊服務系統、彩券機及工業自動化。產
品組合方面(2025)，工業物聯網 48%、智慧工廠與廠務 36%、智慧軟體與方案 16%。銷售區域 (2025)北美 26%、歐洲
37%、中國 9%、其他 28%。主要生產交由母公司鴻海代工，另外樺漢在歐洲新設匈牙利自有工廠，取得當地娛樂、金融
零售、工業網安等訂單，維繫 ODM業務成長。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026年 10月 19日 Ennoconn 第16 頁，共 19頁

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 樺漢整體的 ESG風險評級屬於低風險，於 Sustainalytics資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於領先的位置，
在科技硬體行業中的公司中排名略為領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 樺漢的整體曝險屬於中等水準，略高於科技硬體行業的平均水準。公司較需要改善的  ESG 議
題包含公司資安、商業道德、人力資源等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：樺漢已採取若干措施來管理與重大 ESG議題相關的風險，但這些措施尚不足以涵蓋
其所面臨的所有風險。儘管如此，該公司並未捲入具有重大財務影響的風險事件。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

## Document 207: [000270] 기아(000270) 잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기기아(000270) 잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000270] 기아(000270) 잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기기아(000270) 잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

기아 (000270)

EARNINGS PREVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.10.15  
잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기

3Q26 Preview 
기아는 3Q26E 매출액 31.1조원(+8.2% YoY, -6.0% QoQ), 영업이익 2.2조원
(+53.1% YoY, -14.9% QoQ, OPM 7.2%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회할 전망이다. 
물량은 좋았으나, 아쉬운 믹스와 환율 
글로벌 도매 판매 84.7만대(+7.8% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 83.0만대
(+8.6%)를 기록했다. EV·HEV 확대에 따른 ASP 상승은 지속되고 있다. 전쟁으로 인
한 원자재 상승으로 3분기 재료비 부담은 전분기 대비 3,000억~4,000억원 증가할 
것으로 예상된다. EV 판매 비중은 2분기 13%에서 3분기 15%, 유럽은 35%에서 
43%로 확대되며 유럽 내 마진 희석은 불가피하다. 원화 강세에 따른 영업상 부담은 
판매보증충당부채 환입 등으로 상쇄돼 3분기 환율 영향은 제한적일 전망이다. 판매보
증충당부채 환입은 QoQ +5,000억원 수준으로 추정된다. 4분기부터 환율 효과는 부정
적일 것이며, 환율 10원 변동 시 연간 손익 민감도는 약 1,500억원 수준이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 165,000원으로 하향 
미국 조지아 공장의 텔루라이드 HEV(2.8만대)와 HMGMA의 스포티지 HEV(1.0만대) 
신규 생산으로 북미의 견조한 HEV 수요에 대응하며, 2027년 믹스 개선이 기대된다. 
유럽은 EV3·EV5로 점유율을 확대하는 가운데 유럽 내 할인율 하락과 EV 잔존가치 
회복으로 판매 여건이 개선되고 있다. 향후 IAA 규제에 따른 법인·플릿 혜택 제한은 
중국 업체의 원가 우위를 줄일 수 있을 전망이다. 
원화 약세 지속을 반영해 이익 추정치를 하향하며, 이에 따라 목표주가를 하향한다.  
다만, 현재 주가는 12M Fwd P/E 기준 4.8배로 Legacy OEM 평균(7.7배)보다 낮은 
수준, 저가 매수 기회라 판단된다. 현재 배당 수익률은 6% 수준으로, 상대적으로 높은 
배당 매력은 주가 하방을 지지할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr 
Buy (유지)

## Document 247: AI交易熄火、中东局势恶化，全球股市遭遇“黑色星期四”！

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI交易熄火、中东局势恶化，全球股市遭遇“黑色星期四”！.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

美股盘前，纳斯达克 100 指数期货下跌 1.7% ，特斯拉跌约 5% ，半导体股普跌， IBM 跌约
5% ，英特尔跌约 5% ，美光科技跌约 3% ，英伟达跌约 2% 。光通信股普跌，迈威尔科技跌
约 5% 。布伦特原油⽇内涨幅达 3.0% ，报 112.09 美元 / 桶美元兑⽇元下跌 0.5% ⾄ 157.34 。
现货⻩⾦⽇内跌 1.50% ，现报 4080 美元 / 盎司，现货⽩银失守 56 美元 / 盎司，⽇内跌
3.40% 。
摘要：
科技股财报喜忧参半，叠加中东局势升温，全球市场⻛险情绪降温。
美股盘前，纳斯达克 100 指数期货下跌 1.7% ，本周早些时候积累的 2.2% 涨幅⾯临被⼤幅抹去的
⻛险。特斯拉跌约 5% ，公司三季度营收不及预期，并下调资本开⽀⽬标。半导体股普跌， IBM
跌约 5% ，英特尔跌约 5% ，美光科技跌约 3% ，英伟达跌约 2% 。光通信股普跌，迈威尔科技跌约
5% 。 Meta 微涨，公司 Q3 营收与 Q4 指引双超预期，上调 AI 资本开⽀下限，拟发⾏⽆投票权新

股。
欧洲斯托克 50 指数开盘跌 1.2% ，德国 DAX 指数跌 0.7% ，英国富时 100 指数跌 0.3% ，法国 CAC
40 指数跌 0.9% 。爱⻢仕股价下跌 6% ，公司三季度销售额不及预期。韩国综合指数跌幅⼀度扩⼤
⾄ 4% ， SK 海⼒⼠跌逾 6% ，三星电⼦跌逾 4% ，⽇经 225 指数跌约 2.4% ， MSCI 亚太指数跌
1.5% ，创 7 ⽉底以来最低收盘⽔平。

## Document 255: Allianz_2026-10-08_From legacy capital to smart bets Europe's path to a competitive auto industry_a5c640fb

Source PDF: all report pdf/Allianz_2026-10-08_From legacy capital to smart bets Europe's path to a competitive auto industry_a5c640fb.pdf
Report source: Allianz Trade
Date: 2026-10-08
Pages: 16
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

In Summary

• Chinese brands will dominate  the Paris auto show  next week . They represent 50% of 
exhibitors versus just 23% in 2024, a visible sign of a deeper, structural shift already 
reshaping the market.  China holds 10% of European registrations, 16% in battery electric 
vehicles (BEV) and 26% in plug-in hybrid electric vehicle s (PHEV). This comes as Europe's 
auto market defies a sluggish growth outlook, up +5% YTD through August, suggesting 
structural change rather than a cyclical blip. Low -carbon demand leads the charge: BEV 
sales are up nearly +45% YTD, reaching parity with petrol-powered engine s (22%), amid 
energy volatility, EV cost compression and targeted subsidies for low incomes. 
• A deeper rift is opening between political ambition and industrial execution, and it risks 
setting a technology trap. Easing targets now breeds complacency precisely when the shift 
to low-carbon production needs to accelerate. Europe is poised to soften carbon penalties 
and cut the 2035 zero -emission target to 90%, relieving Original Equipment Manufacturers 
(OEMs) who underinvested rather than forcing them to catch up. Support was needed to 
share the burden, but without a binding roadmap, automakers will simply buy time, falling 
further behind Chinese rivals. Demand itself is uneven : BEVs are today the second biggest 
segment in France and Germany (30 and 26%), yet lag below 10% in Italy, Spain or even in 
the key but less accessible US market, exposing a dangerous short -term versus long -term 
arbitrage. 
• Added-value erosion of EUR3bn by 2030 must be urgently contained. The shift in dominant 
technology and rising competition from China have eroded the big three German carmakers' 
market share materially in core markets – down 10pps in Europe and 7pps in China since 
2021. In Europe, where manufacturing costs are less competitive, domestic added-value per 
vehicle in H1 2026 was 30-45% lower than in 2021/2022 while in China that decline has been 
smaller, less than 20% over the same period. A further realistic 3pps market share slide in 
both markets by 2030 would translate into a EUR3bn added-value deterioration for German 
OEMs, equivalent to 4pps of EBITDA margin. 
• Whoever secures reliable, cost-competitive battery production will shape the next decade 
of automotive competitiveness . Vehicle value is migrating from the factory floor to the 
software stack: Engine and chassis once represented 35 –45% of an internal combustion 
engine (ICE) car's value, but battery and software now account for 50 –60% of a BEV's. 
"Made-in-Europe" content requirements would preserve some manufacturing jobs, but at a 
steep cost: ~ EUR2,000 per vehicle (+5% retail price), coverage of only ~50% of domestic 
battery demand by 2035 and a wider technology gap with global peers. They would do little 
to reduce sovereign risk either: over 70% of European-based battery technology will be held 
by Asian firms , according to the current pipeline . The more achievable path is to grow 
Europe's footprint in capital -light, high -value segments (design and IP ), capturing added 
value without the capital risk of manufacturing.  
• Europe should pursue smart partnerships over full sovereignty, anchoring Asian battery 
and chip production on European soil, pooling procurement and R&D and consolidating 
onto fewer EV platforms under strict capital discipline. Diversifying further into  into 
defense looks relevant it has strong public funding but is running out of capacity . The long-
term bets worth making are solid-state batteries, recycling, proprietary chip design, local AI 
infrastructure and autonomous trucking and logistics, where Europe can differentiate 
without competing head on against entrenched US and Chinese leaders.

Guillaume Dejean  
Senior Sector Adv isor 
guillaume.dejean@allianz -
trade.com

Ano Kuhanathan  
Head of Corporate Research  
ano.kuhanathan@allianz -
trade.com

Allianz Research | 8 October 2026 
From legacy capital to smart bets : Europe's path to 
a competitive auto industry

### Page 2

2

A legacy capital mindset behind a structural crisis 
The 2026 Paris auto show  is emblematic of the changing global order  in the automotive industry . European 
brands account for just 40% of exhibitors in this year’s edition of the flagship industry event , to be held from 12-18 
October, down from 68% compared to 2022 and 2024. This pullback reflects intense cost-cutting measures amid a 
severe profitability crisis, with manufacturers engaging in tactical arbitrage. Meanwhile, Chinese players are seizing 
the opportunity and confirming their strong interest in the European market: Chinese brands surged from 9% to 50% 
of exhibitors at the event, and Chinese OEMs from 15% to a dominant 63% by 2026, using this prime European stage 
to attract new audiences and accelerate their continental expansion. This highlights the rebalancing of power within 
the automotive industry, where dynamic and innovative new competitors are openly contesting the long -held 
dominance of Europe's established giants. While this is  not the industry's first downturn, this time the weakness is 
structural rather than cyclical, and its root is identifiable: a legacy capital mindset that, for two decades, funded the 
defense of mature ICE assets and short -term margins instead of the electric, digital and industrial bets that would 
determine the next cycle. The technology lag, the China shock and the brand erosion are best read not as separate 
failures but as symptoms of that same underlying capital allocation culture.

Figures 1 & 2 : Breakdown of attendees of Paris auto show by  origin of OEMS and brands  /  
12-month trailing revenue, profits and margin rate from largest German automakers

Sources: Salon de l’Auto notice, LSEG Datastream, Allianz Research

A legacy capital mindset: capital defended the past, chased the quarter and bet small when it finally moved.  
European automakers' balance sheets tell the story of a capital culture built for a different era. For years, capital 
was steered to sustain and optimize mature ICE platforms and defend near -term margins – the sunk-cost logic of 
an incumbent reluctant to cannibalize its own cash cow – rather than to pre-fund the electric and digital transition 
while there was still runway to do it gradually. When bets were finally placed, they were sized and governed like 
legacy capex, not like venture-style industrial transitions: The ill-fated decision to internalize middleware software 
development, a domain far removed from automakers' core competencies, burned time and money for negligible 
results, while billions in diesel -related penalties and surging impairment charges drained c ash reserves that could 
instead have funded the transition of ICE plants to EV production lines, scaled battery gigafactories or fueled critical 
R&D. Historically, the auto sector led capex growth during expansion years but proved more vulnerable in 
downturns. Yet today's situation is uniquely alarming: The broader economic cycle remains steady while auto capex 
is collapsing ( -20%, its strongest contraction since 2018) even as non -auto European blue-chip capex expands by 
+10%. That is short-termism showing up directly in the numbers: capital retreating precisely when software-defined 
vehicles, AI -powered autonomous systems and the early diffusion of robotaxis are reshaping the industry's  
competitive frontier. Having already missed the EV wave and fallen behind in the software revolution, European 
carmakers cannot afford to sit out the autonomous race. Yet the pause on Level 3 development is another example 
of risk -averse, low -conviction capital deployment at exactly the wrong moment. In this industry, moats and

technology edges erode fast. Today's investments are tomorrow's profits, and a mindset built to defend yesterday's 
assets has left financial leeway too thin to allow another misjudgment. 
Figure 3 : Evolution of capex from European automakers vs. Stoxx 600 benchmark (annual growth & share)

Sources:  LSEG Datastream, Allianz Research  
The second structural weakness is a  late EV transition.  European OEMs didn't just misjudge the pace of 
electrification, they kept capital deployed to defend ICE production and margins for as long as the balance sheet 
allowed, leaving too little invested when the pivot could no longer be delayed. Electrification diffused faster than 
they bet on: BEVs now account for over 20% of EU registrations (on par with ICE this year) and nearly 50% in China, 
the fastest-growing segment on both continents, and European carmakers are scrambling to catch up. The late 
pivot from ICE to EV and hybrid production is now compressing costs into a brutal timeframe: what could have been 
a gradua l, capital -light transition is instead an expensive, chaotic restructuring. German OEM margins have 
collapsed from 13% in 2022 to roughly 4% today. BEV profitability remains structurally lower than ICE, while Chinese 
competitors deliver advanced technology  at aggressive price points. The same mindset shows up in how setbacks 
were funded: Diesel-gate alone cost Volkswagen over USD34bn, capital that was unavailable to fund a decade of 
electrification R&D instead. The traditional European advantage  – a deep, local supplier ecosystem built on 
mechanical engineering – is eroding fast as EV supply chains shift toward Asian battery and component makers. 
European OEMs may have caught up on sales volumes at home, but their cost structures remain bloated, their 
margins thin and their competitive moat narrower by the quarter.

The third structural weakness is competition from  China: the price of capital that protected margins instead of 
market share.  Over the past decade, China was the biggest and most profitable market for German OEMs, 
accounting for over 30% of group revenue , and capital allocation treated that profit pool as something to protect 
rather than a market worth fighting for with scale. That era is over as the balance of power has been fully disrupted 
by the rise of local players that quickly absorbed foreign brands' market share. The miscalculation was strategic as 
much as financial: European OEMs underestimated Beijing's electrification ambition and the speed at which 
domestic, and in some cases less-experienced, firms would scale, while committing far less capital to matching that 
scale than the threat warranted. Competition has been exported over the past years, with Chinese brands 
redirecting overcapacity mainly towards Europe as high tariffs have almost entirely closed access to the US market. 
Their EU market share surged from about 2% to nearly 10% by mid -2026, totaling over 1mn cars sold on the 
continent for the first time. EU tariffs of up to 35% barely slowed them: Chinese OEMs pivoted to hybrids and PHEVs, 
offered advanced tech at aggressive prices and adopted European -style design. The trade picture tells the rest. 
Europe's historical automotive surplus with China, once peaking near EUR20bn, has flipped into an unprecedented 
deficit. EU auto exports to China halved in two years. In the opposite direction, Chinese EV and hybrid exports to the 
EU doubled to USD14.2bn in H1 2026 alone, with hybrid shipments surging +206%. The deficit is no longer just about 
BEVs; it spans hybrids, PHEVs and components, reflecting the broader penetration of Chinese brands while the 
European footprint in China fades. There is no reason to believe this wave will recede in the near future as OEMs 
intensify relocation of some production capacities in Europe to dodge any trade barrier threat. In the meantime, 
much improvement has been mad e on the after-sales networks and client relationships that remained one of the 
last Achilles' heels of Chinese firms in Europe, and the value proposition is perfectly adapted to European customer 
demand: a good -looking and affordable advanced technology which is precisely the segment European capital 
under-invested in. 
Figures 6 & 7 : Share of imported Chinese brands in Europe vs. exported European brands in China / trade balance 
between EU-27 and China per powertrain breakdown (in EUR bn)

Sources:  Eurostat, CAAM, Allianz Research  
The fourth structural weakness:  Brand power fades once the capital behind the premium stops compounding.  
The aura of German automotive excellence, built on decades of engineering prestige and glossy design , was itself 
a form of capital, accumulated through sustained investment in mechanical engineering. It is cracking now that the 
industry pivots toward electrification and digitalization. The core value in a 2026 vehicle now sits in software-defined 
solutions and battery systems, both firmly controlled by Asian firms, leaving European carmakers defending a 
premium price tag no longer backed by technological superiority. Competitors, notably Chinese brands, deliver a 
sharper performance-to-price ratio, while consumer price sensitivity has surged since the 2022 energy crisis as car -
related costs (i.e. insurance, energy and maintenance) skyrocketed by 20-30% over the core inflation rate since 2020. 
The 2026 EV boom cannot be decoupled from volatile energy prices and aggressive price compression driven by 
fiercer competition and widening supply. This mirrors the broader luxury backlash: heritage alone no longer justifies 
the invoice when the capital that built it was never reinvested in the technology that now defines the category. 
Meanwhile, Europe's most popular segment (i.e. sedans and hatchbacks below EUR50,000) carries a 3 to 5% better 
price-to-range ratio than Chinese BEV models currently sold in Europe yet low export value as different consumer 
tastes and cheaper local alternatives in emerging markets cap revenue upside.  Brand awareness, once the

industry's most durable moat and the clearest return on decades of engineering capital, fades into irrelevance as

capital goes, not incremental adjustment.

Not a premium purchase anymor e? EV adoption is increasingly breaking the psychological glass ceiling that has 
long confined electric cars to an affluent, early -adopter customer base.  The shift from broad -based subsidies 
towards targeted support is making EVs more accessible to mainstream households, while falling vehicle prices and 
improving total cost of ownership are progressively chasing away the perception of EVs as a premium goods. This 
virtuous circle is particularly important for the fleet market: As companies increasingly electrify their fleets, a 
growing volume of relatively young EVs will enter the second -hand market, expanding choice and bringing prices 
within reach of a much broader consumer base. This lifts one of the main structural barriers to adoption so far: the 
limited maturity of the used-EV market and the sharp depreciation suffered by first-generation models, which made 
residual values difficult to predict and leasing costs comparatively high. Energy-price volatility is another factor that 
adds up this year and strengthens the economic case.

### Page 6
